    Homebrew of Pale_Crusader

    01-05-2016, 09:29 PM
    Frailty Chronicles

    Disclaimer*     The follow Material is re-written homebrew adapting rules from Danse Macabre, Mirrors, and Walking Shadows for 2 Edition and general re-balancing

    Lexicon:
    Arcane Beat: Five of these add up to a Arcane Experience, which may be spent on Arcanum, Gnosis, Rotes or Mage Specific Merits. It is gained by following Path/Ministry and Order Aspirations to ones detriment.
    Atrocity: An act that is grievously harmful to an ensoulled being
    Attention: The stat used to track how supernatural creature's accumulated acts of Atrocity make it the target of monster hunting conspiracies, including those run by other supernaturals. It is resisted a character's Cover-Up.
    Bane: A weakness gained by being a Horror, being an Ephemeral Being, having a Major Template having some particular Minor Templates or through acquiring Permanent Atrocity (which are known a Personal Banes and are based on the Atrocity that caused the conversion of Temporary Atrocity into Permanent Atrocity).
    Comfort: An advantage, sometimes in the form of merits other times as positive Conditions, that are provided by a Support.
    Complete Template: A character with both major template and a minor template.
    Cover-up: A stat that reflect a character's effort to conceal themselves from those that would hunt them for committing Atrocities. It is capped by Stability Advantage, and is measured by the lowest of the character's Mental and Social Attributes merits invested in the justification of why the character hasn't been found by monster hunters yet.
    Ensoulled: The quality of having a Stability Advantage as this is the defining characteristic of having a soul, and also moral compunction and objective ethical standing as a self aware creature with free-will. Only Ensoulled beings may have the Soulless Condition, as may things don't have a soul but Soulless actually represents having a place for a soul which is empty. Contrast with Spirits, Slashers with a Greater Undertaking, Strix, Huntsmen, or True Fae to compare intelligent beings solely governed by their nature rather than free-will or agency.
    Fundamental Beat: Five of these add up to a Fundamental Experience, which may be spent on Attributes, or Skills. One is earned for each Chapter attended.
    Frailty Chronicles: Collectedly the chronicles run using these homebrew rules, which are based around promoting gritty, balanced game play where actions have the meaningful consequences, while including mechanics that help police the setting in line with how fluff implies it should be policed, based on the presupposed supernatural conspiracies.
    Kink: A complication in the relationship with a Support, and a source of negative Conditions which provide Sanity Beats when overcome, decreases the blow of using the Support to get rid of Temporary Atrocity.
    Major Template: This is one of the two template slows a character has. The other slot is a minor template, the other is a major template. Having both is considered having a Complete Template. See the Template lists below for more details.
    Minor Template: This is one of the two template slows a character has. The other is a major template. Having both is considered having a Complete Template. See the Template lists below for more details.
    Non-Template: This refers to having neither of the two template slots, major template or minor template, a character filled. Unless the character is a Slasher the character is entirely mundane without inherent supernatural abilities.
    Permanent Atrocity: Is the score used to track how monstrous a supernatural creature is. Acts as a penalty to hiding their supernatural nature and a bonus to overt supernatural feat. It is used to calculate Attention.
    Primal Beat: Five of these add up to a Primal Experience, which may be spent on Gifts, Primal Urge, Rites, or Werewolf Specific Merits. It is gained by following Auspice, Tribe and Pack Aspirations to ones detriment.
    Ossulary Beat: Five of these add up to a Ossulary Experience, which may be spent on Manifestations, Psyche, Ceremonies, or Sin-Eater Specific Merits. It is gained by following Threshold, Archetype, and Krewe Aspirations to ones detriment.
    Resistant: An additional description of Damage that indicates it may not be healed by supernatural means. Is because the damage was acquired via a Bane or was somehow mystically opted into as an action of freewill. It is noted by putting a dot above the affected wound box.
    Sanguine Beat: Five of these add up to a Sanguine Experience, which may be spent on Disciplines, Blood Potency, Devotions, or Vampire Specific Merits. It is gained by following Touchstone, Clan, and Covenant Aspirations to ones detriment.
    Sanity Beat: Five of these add up to a Sane Experience, which may be spent on Supports, or to regain lost Willpower Dots.
    Slasher: Beings with either a Greater and/or Lesser Undertaking. Lesser Undertaking is gained by ensoulled or once ensoulled beings having one Stability Advantage, or less, and Greater Undertaking is gained when such a being is reduced to zero Stability Advantage. Slashers with Greater Undertaking are no longer considered ensoulled. All types of templates including Non-Templates may become Slashers and Slashers track Permanent Atrocity.
    Splat: Inclusive term for all of the Major Templates.
    Stray Beat: Five of these add up to a Stray Experience, which may be spent on Contracts, Wyrd, or Lost Specific Merits. It is gained by following Seeming, Lost, and Court Aspirations to ones detriment.
    Story Beats: Five of these add up to a Story Experience, which may be spent on Skill Specialization or Merits. These are the most common type of beat to gain, and is considered the default type of beat for when a beat type isn't specifically listed.
    Support: A supporting non-template or Minor template character on which a character relies upon for support of their sanity, and the maintenance of this relationship is used as one of the counters in their Stability Advantage. It provides advantages in the forms of Comfort and story complication in the form of Kinks.
    Stability Advantage: The general name to collectively refer to Integrity/Clarity/Harmony/Synergy/Humanity.
    Temporary Atrocity: This represents a character's progress to marking themselves as true monsters. Character gains this from committing an Atrocity, and when they add up to the character's Stability Advantage are trading in for one Permanent Atrocity. This is also used in the calculation for Attention. May be gotten rid with by dumping into a Support relationship in the form of a scene of between the Support and character where a support is confronted by how cruel and frightening the character is becoming, providing the character a chance to see the horror in their Support so that they may change their own hearts.

    Portland Oregon Vampire Politics
    Portland Oregon Changeling Courts



    To see a character sheet showing what these how these house-ruled character's would be recorded follow this link:
    EXAMPLE CHARACTER SHEET

    Please forgive that it is formatted 11x 17, double sided flipping along the short edge, I did that because it is meant to be printed as a booklet with cardstock cover. Also references in the character sheet of "Bulwark" should be read as "Support", the character sheet was formatted before the new changeling rules referenced Bulwarks as a mechanic and I did a name change to avoid confusion.

    Atrocity
    People do horrible things to each other, and sometimes it is more inline with the nightmarish and more severe than the petty bites we regularly inflict on each other everyday. This is domain of Atrocity; murder, maiming for permanent injury, financially ruining, destroying or removing a soul, and torture. Atrocity carries supernatural weight, it scars the world and collective psyche, especially within the World of Darkness. It is this weight that defines the difference between supernatural beings and true monsters and is what these mechanics track. Atrocity as an act earns Temporary Atrocity Points.

    When a character does something ask these questions:

    Does the act grievously harm another ensoulled being?
    If yes add one point of Temporary Atrocity.

    Does the harm affect multiple ensoulled beings?
    If yes add one point of Temporary Atrocity.

    Is the harm functionally irrevocable?
    If yes add one point of Temporary Atrocity.

    Did the character inflicting the harm enjoy it?
    If yes add one point of Temporary Atrocity.

    Was there solid justification for inflicting the harm?
    If yes, and the Storyteller agrees, add one less point of Temporary Atrocity. (A good guideline is the Atrocity should be no worse than what it is punishing or necessary to prevent to qualify for examination to judge if this clause apply.)

    Example that could answer all the question yes:
    Shooting the vampire, who murdered you and your family, in the face until he is truly dead in front of his wife and children. You can do this because you are a Sin-eater and that murder was the cause of your Torn threshold. This would net 3 Temporary Atrocity Points

    Reducing Temporary Atrocity Points
    To reduce Temporary Atrocity points you can once per chapter call for a small narrated scene and roll the character's current total Temporary Atrocity Points as Basic Stress against a Support (if the scene fits the Support's Kink subtract 2 dice from the die pool). If the roll is a success or exceptional success all current Temporary Atrocity is removed from the character, however on an exceptional success still removes all Temporary Atrocity but the Hurt is treated as Lethal instead. If successful and the character has a major, minor or complete template the scene always includes horrific clues of the character's monstrous nature which the Support consciously ignores or denies to themselves. This lays the seed of contempt and terror which is part of the damage to the relationship reflected in the Hurt.

    When a character's Temporary Atrocity points add up to equal a character's Stability Advantage, that character's Temporary Atrocity points are cleared and a Permanent Atrocity Dot is added to the character if the have a Minor Template, Major Template, Complete Template, and or a Major Slasher Undertaking. Non-template characters and is without a Major Slasher Undertaking do not gain Permanent Atrocity. When non-template characters have their Temporary Atrocity Points add up to their Integrity, they clear the Temporary Atrocity Points and lose a Support as if it was due to Aggravated Hurt.

    Affects of Permanent Atrocity
    Atrocity carries supernatural weight, and that weight is filled with tainted power. Monsters are terrifying for good reason, even though they are riddled with flaws and weaknesses in horror stories.

    Each Permanent Atrocity Dot adds a Personal Bane to the character appropriate to how they gained the Permanent Atrocity, increases the severity of all Banes, permanently increases the likelihood of drawing the Attention of Monster Hunters, adds a bonus die to all rolls that use Supernatural powers (This include rolls that have a Skill or Attribute enhanced by a supernatural source) except when attempt to conceal the character's supernatural nature, and a penalty to all rolls to conceal the character's supernatural nature.

    If Attention ever exceeds Cover-Up while in the mundane world gain Survielled Condition

    Attention = Temporary Atrocity + Permanent Atrocity

    Cover-Up is a statistic that represents the effectiveness of concealing one's monstrous nature and activities.

    It is capped by the character's Stability Advantage. A Character's Cover-Up is the amount of Attention they can withstand before they suffer the Survielled or Hunted Conditions.

    Cover-up = Total of Merits Dots used to conceal monstrous nature + lowest Mental or Social Attribute

    Player needs to describe how these merits contribute to their Cover-Up:

    Potentially Applicable Merits:
    Alternate Identity
    Anonymity
    Fame
    Mundane Status
    Resources
    Retainers
    Safe Place

    Beings without Supports automatically gain a dot of Permanent of Atrocity whenever they commit an act of Atrocity that would have net Temporary Atrocity greater than zero. They are also not capped in Cover-Up based on their Stability Advantage. Instead corporeal beings, like horrors, Slashers with a Greater Undertaking, Huntsmen have a cap of 1 cover-up. Ephemeral beings, such as but not limited to spirits and ghosts, have a cap of 3 and uses its Finesse to calculate Cover-Up. These may be modified by how easily an area hides monstrous violence and cruelty. Either may have a +5.

    Example of calculation:
    Vinny, the Mortal Mafia Tough Guy
    Permanent Atrocity: 0 (as a non-template Vinny can't acquire Permanent Atrocity)

    Temporary Atrocity: 2 (Vinny does a lot of bad things but also regularly dumps his Temporary Atrocity into his Support relationship such as his battered girl friend, his long suffering mother, and other relationships that he has lost over the years. He refrains from dumping Temporary Atrocity into some relationships such as his best friend and co-knee-breaker Joe, or his mafia boss Bruno.)

    Integrity: 4 (Integrity is the number of Support the character retains. Vinny has lost several Support relationships because of his habit of dumping his Temporary Atrocity into them, as well as the Hurt caused by the breaking points he violates. His mother, girlfriend, best friend and mafia boss are his 4 remaining Supports.)

    Intelligence: 1 (Vinny isn't particularly bright, and Intelligence is his lowest Mental or Social stat.)

    Merits: Status: Mafia 2 and Alternate Identity 1 (Vinny his standing in the Mafia and his Alias of "Mr. Gray" make sure people don't connect his work to his home life.)

    This adds up to a Cover-Up of 4, and an Attention of 2. This leaves Vinny vulnerable to gaining the Survielled Condition by those looking for monstrous activity were he to commit an Atrocity that would be worth 3 or more Temporary Atrocity. Vinny would do well to drop more Atrocity before coming another Atrocity, but he risks losing more Support, thus Integrity. This is the spiral that leads to becoming a Slasher.

    What's the line between monsters, brutal hitmen or serial killers you ask? Very little when you're investigating to find them.

    Most mundane people have a Cover-Up of 2, (Having 'average' as their lowest Mental or Social Attribute, and no merits specifically applied to conceal Atrocity incurring acts) Strangely people with Mental or Social deficiency, thus a Social or Mental Attribute of 1, are more likely to dedicate merits for hypothetical or actual Atrocity incurring acts, thus raising their average Cover-Up. This is likely caused by emotionally or mentally maladjusted individuals being the most likely to resort to Atrocity. Did this just justify some players' tenancy to make min-maxxed combat-monkeys? Yes, with the caveat that it now comes with mechanical disadvantages, additional point costs, and means they cop to playing an "emotionally or mentally maladjusted individual" for a character.

    The worst monsters (10 Permanent Atrocity) in game need to maintain perfect Stability Advantage, and extensive infrastructure in the form of Merits concealing their monstrous nature, and can't commit a single Atrocity unless it is justified, brings them no pleasure and only harms only one ensoulled being in a way they isn't irrevocable, without being the subject of Monster Hunter Attention.

    At these highest levels of Permanent Atrocity a monster that has painted itself into a corner, such as the iconic dispassionate vampire Prince staking his foes or even a Werewolf that is the emotionally dead and physically scarred Alpha of a massive Pure pack that only beats a forsaken up to make an example of him, but leaves him alive and doesn't outright strip a forsaken pack of their territory even though he could. When you've become this powerful from being a monster you are on a tight-wire and must be more merciful than lesser monsters. Should those examples slip, by losing Merits that make up their Cover-Up, gaining Temporary Atrocity, or losing a Support, they fall into a downward spiral of gaining the Survielled and Hunted Conditions until they die or undo slip.

      Minor Template
      Most Minor Templates are templates which are acquired by choice and dedication, a few are matters of birth. This places Minor Templates into two categories Acquired (which are in the Majority) and Inherited. There are 24 Acquired Minor Templates which at their core are a supernatural expression of a specialization in a skill, with other Acquired Templates having different prerequisites. Acquired Minor Templates always have prerequisites that must be met to gain them, and may be easier for certain types of character than others to fulfill. Even a Non-Template without assistance can gain the Specialization-Based Minor Template if they do what is necessary and have the proper mastery. Specialization-Based Acquired Minor Templates have an association with one of the five Major Template's Supernatural Fuel Sources or with Willpower. Each Supernatural Fuel Source and Willpower each have four associated Specialization-Based Acquired Minor Templates, and their association is either Prominent or Marginal, two of each for each Supernatural Fuel Sources. Specialization-Based Acquired Minor Templates with a Prominent association with a Supernatural Fuel Source may substitute that Supernatural Fuel Source for Willpower in activating the Dread Powers granted by the Minor Template and Willpower to activate some things which normally take the Supernatural Fuel Source, and the Prerequisites for the Major Template which have Pools of that Supernatural Fuel Source (or for Non-Templates in the case of Willpower) are only a single dot in the Skill associate with the Minor Template plus the specific Specialty listed, and members of the Major Template may universally act as Mentors to lower the skill requirements to enter the Minor Template. Minor Template which are only Marginal associated with a Supernatural Fuel Source only benefit from the ability to interchangeably use that Supernatural Fuel Source for Willpower in activating the Dread Powers granted by the Minor Template and Willpower to activate things which normally take the Supernatural Fuel Source, and have a higher level of Prerequisites in general.

      Each Specialization-Based Acquired Minor Template is keyed to a Skill and a specific Specialization. The Specialization is non-negotiable, but the levels of skill needed vary depending if it is Prominently Associated with the Supernatural Fuel Sources of the Major Template, members of the Major Template (or Non-Template in the case of Willpower) only require 1 dot in the skill, those apprentice under a Mentor of the Major Template with a Prominent association require 2 dots, apprenticing under an existing Member of the Minor Template which doesn't qualify for the Prominent discount is 3 dots, and those without any guidance or innate Template association require 4 dots.

      Some Minor Templates are exclusive to members of Major Templates, such as Bloodlines for Vampires, Entitlements for Changelings, Legacies for Mages, Lodges for Werewolves, and Traditions for Sin-Eaters. Only members of those Major Templates may gain those Minor Templates and only if they meet the requirements.

      Each Minor Template which isn't exclusive to a particular Major Template gains a special type of Aspiration called an Appetite. If being with a Minor Template doesn't fulfill their Appetite each Month, then they cumulatively lose access to a Willpower Dot for each month they abstain and gain the Deprived Condition. If deprived being with Minor Template fulfills their Appetite then they immediately regain access to the Willpower Dots they've been denied access due to abstaining from their Appetite and resolve the Deprived Condition. Fulfilling Appetites replenish Willpower points spent instead of providing beats, exactly as Feeding Aspirations do with Horrors. Appetites count as Aspirations for purposes of Social Maneuvering and anything else an Aspiration would, except for grant Beats or Experience.

      --To ban/bane or not to ban/bane?--
      You'll notice some Minor Templates may or may not have a Bane and/or Ban, and wonder why? They are in place along with the severity of their Appetite to balance them against other Minor Templates in terms of unequal Dread Powers.
      -- --


      Essence
      These templates are Essence-based, and associated with the savage world of the Uratha and Spirits, where eat or be eaten drives a brutal ecology of constant change. It is governed by the principles of delicate balance, predation, and metaphoric ecology. Minor Template in this category may purchase Common Disciplines at Out of Clan prices, and qualify to buy any Merits which are Wolf-Blooded Specific. This also applies to Uratha Specific Merits at ST discretion.


      Lurking Terror
      There are places people are not meant to go, and such places tend to gain guardians, beings which become timeless legends due to their role. These guardians are Lurking Terrors, tied
      Becoming a Lurking Terror:
      A Lurking Terror requires finding an abandoned location which is mystically charged, it could be a Locus, a haunted location, a Verge, an Iris, Hallow, Gate to some other realm, or other less easily categorized Strange Place. The Lurking Terror-to-be (which uses the equipment building rules page CoD 100, Resolve + Intimidation if the crafting is done under pressure in downtime) must lay claim to the location and vow to eternally keep people away. After the claiming and vow, the Lurking Terror-to-be spends a Willpower Dot to focus their dedication and immediately loses a Dot of their Stability Advantage, the relationship being devoured by the location.
      The mechanical prerequisites to become a Lurking Terror vary in the following ways
      If the character is a Uratha, Host or Claimed, they require Intimidation 1 and a Skill Specialization Ominous Territory. This is because becoming a Lurking Terror is innately intuitive for any of those that are already halfway between spirit and flesh, and the little remaining is the mastery represent in that skill and specialty.
      If the person is not one of the above exceptions, they require a Social Merit representing the relationship to the person who is already a Lurking Terror which is instructing the character in how to become an Lurking Terror (even if the Lurking Terror is another Player Character or Support, though this is often done as a Mentor relationship, there is flexibility in which social merit is most appropriate), Intimidation 2 and a Skill Specialization Ominous Territory.
      Someone who isn’t already a vampire and has no relationship to guide them may still stumble across the methods of becoming an Lurking Terror but require Intimidation 4 and a Skill Specialization Ominous Territory.
      Tags Gained: Faithful
      Essential Will: May spend Essence instead of Willpower to use Dread Powers, and may spend Willpower instead of other Essence when using a Gift. This does not grant an Essence Pool, which must be acquired from another source for this to be applicable for the character.
      Potency: The character gains one dot of Potency as if he were a Horror, this affect Willpower Capacity, and Supernatural Tolerance. For those with a Major Template the Supernatural Tolerance does stack with that granted by the Major Template’s Power Stat.
      Territorial: Every month a Lurking Terror goes without fulfilling their Appetite she loses access to a Willpower Dot, but upon fulfilling the Lurking Terror’s Appetite she regains access to all the Willpower Dots she lost access to, in the state they were lost in(filled with Willpower Points or empty). Lurking Terror also gain Willpower points by fulfilling her Appetite. Unlike Aspirations, Appetite does not grant Beats.
      Appetite: Keeping area defined by Territory Dread Power vacant of Non-Templates for a Month
      Bane: Being outside of the area defined by One with Territory Dread Power
      Dread Powers:
      One with Territory: The Lurking Terror has a specific form of territory on which he gains supernatural bonuses. This is always the supernatural location where he gained this Template, and does not extend beyond the boundaries of the supernatural location. While on its territory, the Lurking Terror adds three dice to all its physical dice pools and supernatural power rolls, reduces all damage suffered by three, and gains a +3 bonus to its rolls to resist supernatural effects. The Lurking Terror is also immune to any adverse affects of the location, mystical or otherwise, including any Environmental Tilts.
      Legendary: The Lurking Terror isn’t bound by mortal laws of life and death. It’s not indestructible, per se, but if it’s killed by anything other than its Bane, he just comes back in a later scene like nothing happened. Even if it’s destroyed with its Bane, he’s never really gone, a new group of Non-Templates exploring the location will resurrect the Lurking Terror if the place is intact, even if the location was destroyed to get rid of the Lurking Terror recreating the location can always bring the Lurking Terror back for a sequel story.
      Sense Trespassers: The Lurking Terror has incredibly honed senses for a specific type of prey. Against that prey — those trespassing in his territory — the Lurking Terror gains a +4 bonus to all Perception rolls, and applies 9-Again to the dice pool. He can also preternaturally sense the location of Non-Templates within the territory without perception checks.
      ss bypass this power.

      Primordial Predator
      In the beginning, before the rise of the Gauntlet, the world of the flesh and the world of the spirit were intertwined. In that time immemorial creatures have stalked between the worlds with ease, and as a remnant of that epoch Primordial Predators still do. Primordial Predators tap into the wilderness left in the world and it transforms them, making them capable of traveling between worlds.
      Becoming a Primordial Predator:
      A Primordial Predator requires a pilgrimage deep into the wilderness, equipped only for primative survival, with an intension to perform either a vision quest or otherwise commune with nature. The Primordial Predator-to-be (which uses the equipment building rules page CoD 100, Resolve + Survival if the crafting is done under pressure in downtime) must spend 4 days and nights without civilized contact and then must stumble across an Open Verge to the Hisil. After passing through, the Primordial Predator-to-be will feel a chance to connect to the world like with was when it was more primordial, giving him the chance to spend a Willpower Dot to focus their mind on doing just that and immediately loses a Dot of their Stability Advantage as their body jarringly transforms.
      The mechanical prerequisites to become a Primordial Predator vary in the following ways
      They standardly require a Social Merit representing the relationship to the person who is already a Primordial Predator which is instructing the character in how to become a Primordial Predator (even if the Primordial Predator is another Player Character or Support, though this is often done as a Mentor relationship, there is flexibility in which social merit is most appropriate), Survival 3 and a Skill Specialization Pouncing down on Prey. Someone who has no relationship to guide them may still stumble across the methods of becoming a Monk but require Survival 4 and a Skill Specialization Pouncing down on Prey.
      Tags Gained: Shapeshifter
      Essential Will: May spend Essence instead of Willpower to use Dread Powers, and may spend Willpower instead of other Essence when using a Gift. This does not grant an Essence Pool, which must be acquired from another source for this to be applicable for the character.
      Potency: The character gains one dot of Potency as if he were a Horror, this affect Willpower Capacity, and Supernatural Tolerance. For those with a Major Template the Supernatural Tolerance does stack with that granted by the Major Template’s Power Stat.
      Heart of a Predator: Every month a Primordial Predator goes without fulfilling their Appetite they lose access to a Willpower Dot, but upon fulfilling the Primordial Predator’s Appetite he regains access to all the Willpower Dots they lost access to, in the state they were lost in (filled with Willpower Points or empty). Skyline Stalkers also gain Willpower points by fulfilling their Appetite. Unlike Aspirations, Appetite does not grant Beats.
      Appetite: Spend a week successfully hunting in the Hisil.
      Dread Powers:
      Savage Fang and Talons: The Primordial Predator is armed with formidable natural weaponry, both savage jaws, and rending talons. The both types of attacks have a weapon modifier and armorpiercing quality 2. The Primordial Predator does not need to establish a grapple in order to bite. These fangs and talons are present in all solid material forms the Primordial Predator has, and for forms that already have bite and claws the weapon modifier and armorpiercing quality increase by 2.
      Primordial Verge: The Primordial Predator can turn a structure or densely overgrown area into something that defies euclidean understanding of space, creating an insane maze which is also a Verge to the Hisil. By spending 3 Willpower and touching a vertical surface, it turns the area into a tangled mess of corridors, rooms, paths and clearings that lead back on themselves; this effect lasts for an hour. Anyone other than the Primordial Predator who attempts to move through the area must succeed at a Wits + Composure check minus the Primordial Predator’s Resolve each time they attempt to leave the area or progress through it to somewhere specific; if they fail they are unable to progress. If victims of this power fail even a single time while navigating when the Dread Power ends they are on the side of Gauntlet of the Primordial Predator's choosing.

      Skinchanger
      Becoming Skinchanger requires forging an intense bond with an animal and then killing it in a way that leaves its skin intact and after its death attempting to climb into the body or otherwise under its skin. The process of bonding is vital to the process (which uses the equipment building rules page CoD 100, Presence + Animal Ken if the crafting is done under pressure). Upon completing the gruesome task of attempting to climb into their dead companion, the Skinchanger-to-be spends a Willpower Dot and immediately loses a Dot of their Stability Advantage to dedicate themselves to the exercise.
      The mechanical prerequisites to become a Skinchanger vary in the following ways
      If the character is a Uratha, Host or Claimed, they require Animal Ken 1 and a Skill Specialization related to the first animal victim. This is because becoming a Skinchanger is innately intuitive for any of those that are already halfway between spirit and flesh, and the little remaining is the mastery represent in that skill and specialty.
      If the person is not one of the above exceptions, they require a Social Merit representing the relationship to the person who is already a Skinchanger which is instructing the character in how to become an Skinchanger (even if the Skinchanger is another Player Character or Support, though this is often done as a Mentor relationship, there is flexibility in which social merit is most appropriate), Animal Ken 2 and a Skill Specialization related to the first animal victim.
      Someone who isn’t already a vampire and has no relationship to guide them may still stumble across the methods of becoming an Skinchanger but require Animal Ken 4 and a Skill Specialization related to the first animal victim
      Tags Gained: Shapeshifter
      Essential Will: May spend Essence instead of Willpower to use Dread Powers, and may spend Willpower instead of other Essence when using a Gift. This does not grant an Essence Pool, which must be acquired from another source for this to be applicable for the character.
      Potency: The character gains one dot of Potency as if he were a Horror, this affect Willpower Capacity, and Supernatural Tolerance. For those with a Major Template the Supernatural Tolerance does stack with that granted by the Major Template’s Power Stat.
      Itch to change skins: Every month a Skinchanger goes without fulfilling their Appetite she loses access to a Willpower Dot, but upon fulfilling the Skinchanger’s Appetite she regains access to all the Willpower Dots she lost access to, in the state they were lost in(filled with Willpower Points or empty). Skinchanger also gain Willpower points by fulfilling her Appetite. Unlike Aspirations, Appetite does not grant Beats.
      Appetite: Use the Stolen-Skin Dread power on a new victim.
      Dread Powers:
      Beastmaster: The Skinchanger has mastery over lesser beasts. By spending 1 Willpower, it can conjure up a swarm of vermin, small animals, fish, or birds as appropriate to its current Stolen-Skin. These animals will obey its commands, and it can communicate with them clearly. It can also use this power on a single larger animal.
      Stolen-Skin: The Skinchanger can steal the face and skin of another while within its Human Facade Dread Power. It must
      kill the victim first, then spend 2 Willpower and merge the features of the corpse with his own. The change is permanent; this becomes the new form that replaces the previous Stolen-Skin, he cannot return to an earlier face and appearance. While in that form use the all Stats of the Victim and none of their own Stats except the Human Facade Dread Power. Note this power is often used on animals and if the form is not humanoid do not calculate breaking points until resuming the Human Facade. Please note abilities to return something to its "true form" will return them to their current Stolen Skin. Changing to the Stolen-Skin from the Human Facade is free and requires no roll but takes an Instant Action.
      Human Facade: This Dread Power disguises a Skinchanger as a human as they were before taking this Template. Using the Dread Power costs 1 Willpower, and the human facade seeming lasts the power is deactivated, by the Skinchanger, by someone countering this dread power, or by the Skinchanger's death. The human “costume” is flawed — witnesses may make a Wits
      + Composure roll penalized by the Skinchanger’s Manipulation to realize that something is wrong. While in this Facade the character has access to all their normal stats.

      Storytelling Hints: The profound relationship with the first animal victim, the extremely intense and morose acts of killing it and attempting to climb inside it has a strong trend of drawing individuals that view this template as a way to become an animal. Strictly speaking the powers of this minor template work just as well on humans and supernatural beings, and since Stolen-Skin, having the drawback of changing the forms is by necessity of fulfilling their Itch to change Skins a temporary proposition and makes long term impersonation of humans or supernatural not sustainable. Stolen-Skin a series of animals whose primary benefits are not their identities has many advantages to consistently Skin-Taking a beings whose are useful mostly only because of their identity and that has to be discarded in less than a year. The most successful skinchangers understand Skin-Taking in ways that are Atrocities is something to resort to only when it can’t be avoided because it draws far more attention than preying upon animals or even spirits.

      Skyline Stalker
      Legends of monsters coming from the night sky to swoop away travelers, medieval gargoyles, certain comic book heroes leaping from rooftop to rooftop, these archetypes all tap into the fear caused by a prey instinct, something even the first mammals would have been familiar with: merciless death come suddenly and unseen when it is from above. It is a twitching reflexive paranoia that doesn't make sense to most modern people whom presume the world is sane and humans are the top of the food chain. Those humans are wrong, of course, and anyone who knows about the Skyline Stalkers know a paranoid upward glance can have the chance to detect these lofty hunters before they strike.
      Becoming a Skyline Stalker:
      A Skyline Stalker requires an obsession with stalking the night, and swooping down upon prey from above. The Skyline Stalker-to-be (which uses the equipment building rules page CoD 100, Resolve + Athletics if the crafting is done under pressure in downtime) must stalk, kill, and devour the flesh of ensouled prey by dropping down upon it from above. After the feast of flesh, the Skyline Stalker-to-be spends a Willpower Dot to focus their mind on tasting the victim's surprise and immediately loses a Dot of their Stability Advantage from their profane enjoyment of the blasphemous meal.
      The mechanical prerequisites to become a Skyline Stalker vary in the following ways
      They standardly require a Social Merit representing the relationship to the person who is already a Skyline Stalker which is instructing the character in how to become a Skyline Stalker (even if the Skyline Stalker is another Player Character or Support, though this is often done as a Mentor relationship, there is flexibility in which social merit is most appropriate), Athletics 3 and a Skill Specialization Pouncing down on Prey. Someone who has no relationship to guide them may still stumble across the methods of becoming a Monk but require Athletics 4 and a Skill Specialization Pouncing down on Prey.
      Tags Gained: Vampire
      Essential Will: May spend Essence instead of Willpower to use Dread Powers, and may spend Willpower instead of other Essence when using a Gift. This does not grant an Essence Pool, which must be acquired from another source for this to be applicable for the character.
      Potency: The character gains one dot of Potency as if he were a Horror, this affect Willpower Capacity, and Supernatural Tolerance. For those with a Major Template the Supernatural Tolerance does stack with that granted by the Major Template’s Power Stat.
      Raptor's Hunger: Every month a Skyline Stalker goes without fulfilling their Appetite they lose access to a Willpower Dot, but upon fulfilling the Sky Stalker’s Appetite he regains access to all the Willpower Dots they lost access to, in the state they were lost in (filled with Willpower Points or empty). Skyline Stalkers also gain Willpower points by fulfilling their Appetite. Unlike Aspirations, Appetite does not grant Beats.
      Appetite: Devouring an Aggravated Health Level worth of Flesh from an ensouled prey that you slain by pouncing from upon high on it.
      Dread Powers:
      Scale Sheer Surfaces: The Skyline Stalker can walk up walls or even cling to the ceiling, and can move its full Speed while doing so. With free hands the Skyline Stalker can land from a jump made via his Feathery Flesh Dread Power on a wall or ceiling.
      Feathery Flesh: The creature can make great, bounding leaps; by spending 1 Willpower, it can jump about four stories straight up or across a six-lane highway (or the equivalent distance). This power also allows the Skyline Stalker to Reflexively spend a Willpower to negate any damage he takes during a fall, but not the damage his impact makes on a target.
      Stony Skin: The creature is nearly indestructible. Any attack that does not score an exceptional success inflicts only a single point of bashing damage. Exceptional successes inflict damage as normal. If the creature has a weakness of some sort, attacks incorporating that weakne


      Glamour
      These templates are Glamour-based, and associated with the treacherous world of the fae, where few things are as them seem and nothing is as you expect. It is governed by the principles of Fate, narrative conventions, and binding interpersonal concords. Minor Template in this category may purchase Goblin Contracts, and qualify to buy any Merits which are Fae Specific.


      Fae Courtesan
      Ethereally sublime Fae Courtesans host parties so amazing, awesome and terrible that they frequently drive a sane person mad. They can grant mind bending pleasure with a simple smiling glance from across the room, a sweet whispered nothing, or subtle touch of a finger. They are the beating heart through which the fae life blood of gossip and rumors flow.
      Becoming a Fae Courtesan:
      Opting into the role of being a Fae Courtesan requires a cultivated preternatural pretension, posh networking potent enough to strangling a Nemean lion, and hosting a Fae get together that drives a mortal guest of honor insane. The Fae-Courtesan-to-be (which uses the equipment building rules page CoD 100, Composure + Socialize if the crafting is done under pressure) must host this fae gathering in a location in the mundane world with a Dance and Ball Contrivance Condition and is Open or greater. Upon the non-template guest of honor gaining a Broken, Madness, or Fugue Condition from a breaking point the Fae-Courtesan-to-be spends a Willpower Dot and immediately loses a Dot of their Stability Advantage to seal their commitment to the role.
      The mechanical prerequisites to become a Fae Courtesan vary in the following ways
      They standardly require a Social Merit representing the relationship to the person who is already a Fae Courtesan which is instructing the character in how to become a Fae Courtesan (even if the Fae Courtesan is another Player Character or Support, though this is often done as a Mentor relationship, there is flexibility in which social merit is most appropriate), Socialize 3 and a Skill Specialization Fae Gatherings. Someone who has no relationship to guide them may still stumble across the methods of becoming a Fae Courtesan but require Socialize 4 and a Skill Specialization Fae Gatherings.
      Tags Gained: Fae
      Glamorous Will: May spend Glamour instead of Willpower to use Dread Powers, and may spend Willpower instead of Glamour to use Contracts. This does not grant a Glamour Pool, which must be acquired from another source for this to be applicable for the character.
      Potency: The character gains one dot of Potency as if he were a Horror, this affect Willpower Capacity, and Supernatural Tolerance. For those with a Major Template the Supernatural Tolerance does stack with that granted by the Major Template’s Power Stat.
      Won't you come to my tea-party?: Every month a Fae Courtesan goes without fulfilling their Appetite they lose access to a Willpower Dot, but upon fulfilling the Fae Courtesan’s Appetite he regains access to all the Willpower Dots they lost access to, in the state they were lost in (filled with Willpower Points or empty). Fae Courtesan also gain Willpower points by fulfilling their Appetite. Unlike Aspirations, Appetite does not grant Beats.
      Appetite: Host a formal fae gathering with a non-template guest of honor, whom suffers a breaking point because of their attendance.
      Dread Powers:
      Stunning Appearance:[/b] At any gathering a Fae Courtesan can spend a single Instant Action socializing with someone there and rolls Presence + Socialize + Supernatural Tolerance to activate this Dread Power. The interaction on the Fae Courtesan part can be a handshake, bow, introduction or as simple as a smile, a nod, wink or any other "moment" that can be shared between two people. This power then causes momentary irrational paralysis in anyone who can see the Fae Courtesan due to their terrific presence. This Dread Power costs 3 Willpower, and its activation is contested with Presence + Composure + Supernatural Tolerance individually by anyone looking at the Fae Courtesan. Anyone achieving fewer successes than the Fae Courtesan is unable to move or speak for a turn. If the Fae Courtesan gains an exceptional success, the effect lasts three turns.
      Sensually Sublime:[/b] The Fae Courtesan forces a response from the pleasure centers of a living being’s brain, granting ecstatic visions, a feeling of communion with the universe, and sensations of bliss. The Dread Power costs 2 Willpower and an Instant Action Presence + Socialize roll representing personal social contact to activate. The target must be able to sense the Fae Courtesan with one of the five senses for this power to work (A target smelling a handkerchief-favor or tasting a locket of hair allows it to be used over the phone call, or hand-written letters with the scent cross time as well as space, written social interactions only count for the first reader who is affected but sound and visual recordings do not count however as they are mere representations) If successful, the victim suffers the Insensate Tilt. If the victim fails a Resolve + Supernatural Tolerance roll, she gains a Obsessed Condition (Pleasing that particular Fae Courtesan) for the Fae Courtesan’s Presence in days, which binds her thoughts to anticipate and fulfill the Fae Courtesan’s wishes.
      Twisting the Masques:[/b] The Fae Courtesan by veiling and unveiling the target's eyes or ears with a Dexterity + Socialize Roll may create illusions by twisting how fae Masks are experienced by that single target; Anyone or anything with a Mask can be altered in appearance, sound, smell, taste, and touch to the perception of the victim according to the will of the Fae Courtesan. Masks which are seen through don't count for the purpose of this power. The Dread Power costs 1 Willpower and is contested by the victim’s Wits + Composure + Supernatural Tolerance. Duration is 10 minutes per success.

      Fae Tinker
      If you understand the world of the fae you understand the world is made not of atoms but of stories, and sprites, hobs, True Fae, and even some powerful Lost need Contrivances to survive in the material world and even those that don't need them can still wield them as potent tools. Fate marks the world based on how the story has unfolded so far, which shapes what will come to pass. Fae Tinkers have peeked behind the curtain of a particular aspect of reality within some of these stories: Mac Guffins. The persuit to own, claim or deny others many mystical or even valuable mundane items are the central driving force of quite a few stories. These items have the Mac Guffin Contrivance Condition, and Fae Tinkers have special powers over such items.
      Becoming a Fae Tinker:
      Opting into the role of being a Fae Tinker require crafting an object that attracts the attention of people and gains the Mac Guffin Contrivance Condition for being the central motivating force of some a Fate touched plot. The Fae-Tinker-to-be (which uses the equipment building rules page CoD 100, Resolve + Craft if the crafting is done under pressure) must make this object in a location in the mundane world with a Contrivance Condition suitable to the origin point of the particular Mac Guffin and is Open or greater. Upon completing the crafting the Fae-Tinker-to-be spends a Willpower Dot and immediately loses a Dot of their Stability Advantage.
      The mechanical prerequisites to become a Fae Tinker vary in the following ways
      If the person is a Lost, Hob, or True Fae they require Craft 1 and a Skill Specialization Mystical Trinkets. This is because becoming a Fae Tinker is innately intuitive for any of those that are already fae, and the little remaining is the mastery represent in that skill and specialty. If the person is not already fae, they require a Social Merit representing the relationship to the person who is already a Fae Tinker which is instructing the character in how to become a Fae Tinker (even if the Fae Tinker is another Player Character or Support, though this is often done as a Mentor relationship, there is flexibility in which social merit is most appropriate), Craft 2 and a Skill Specialization Mystical Trinkets. Someone who isn’t already a fae and has no relationship to guide them may still stumble across the methods of becoming a Fae Tinker but require Craft 4 and a Skill Specialization Mystical Trinkets.
      Tags Gained: Fae
      Glamorous Will: May spend Glamour instead of Willpower to use Dread Powers, and may spend Willpower instead of Glamour to use Contracts. This does not grant a Glamour Pool, which must be acquired from another source for this to be applicable for the character.
      Potency: The character gains one dot of Potency as if he were a Horror, this affect Willpower Capacity, and Supernatural Tolerance. For those with a Major Template the Supernatural Tolerance does stack with that granted by the Major Template’s Power Stat.
      Appetite for breaking plot devices: Every month a Fae Tinker goes without fulfilling their Appetite they lose access to a Willpower Dot, but upon fulfilling the Fae Tinker’s Appetite he regains access to all the Willpower Dots they lost access to, in the state they were lost in (filled with Willpower Points or empty). Fae Tinkers also gain Willpower points by fulfilling their Appetite. Unlike Aspirations, Appetite does not grant Beats.
      Appetite: Destroy a Mac Guffin because it empowers your mastery over them.
      Dread Powers:
      Strenthen Mac Guffin: The Fae Tinker can enhance an object with the Mac Guffin Contrivance Condition with a Crafts + Wits Instant Action. It can make an object more robust, gaining the Fae Tinker's Potency + any other Supernatural Tolerance in Structure. This ability can shift any Mac Guffen Contrivance Condition to Open for its duration, which in the case of Mac Guffin Hedgegates makes them passable for that period. Duration is 10 minutes per success. The cost is 1 Willpower.
      Manipulate Mac Guffin: The Fae Tinker can make minor changes to the abilities of objects with the Mac Guffin Contrivance Condition, such as slightly changing the nature of the Mac Guffin's abilities or target of an object’s functionality with a Crafts + Intelligence Instant Action which takes a minute of being able to handle the object. Slight changes should not alter the overall scope, scale, or severity of the power and should still fit story conventions the other Contrivances the Mac Guffin object; If Mac Guffin is the only contrivance Condition upon the object it gains Artifact of Attraction Contrivance for the duration of this ability and while the Mac Guffin has that Contrivance it gives everyone (except Fae Tinkers) viewing it the Obsessed Condition (Claim that particular Mac Guffin), which must be resolved normally. Changing the target of the functionality may be done at will as an single round Instant Action at anytime during the duration. Duration is 1 hour per success. The cost is 2 Willpower.
      Seek Mac Guffin: The Fae Tinker can sense the presence of the Mac Guffin Contrivance Condition from a distance. The base range is two miles per the Fae Tinker's Potency + any other Supernatural Tolerance; Fae Tinkers may spend an Willpower to multiply this by 10. If successful on a Wits roll, the entity becomes aware of the direction and distance to the nearest Mac Guffin Contrivance Condition.

      Goblin
      Cruelly dealing with mankind to crush their hopes and dreams by offering them ill-begotten bargains is the quintessence of being a Goblin. Those that are Goblin opt into the role by binding themselves as actors in the tropes “Careful what you wish for”, “Deal with the Devil”, and “Want of a nail”. Many Goblins are Hobs, Lost and even True Fae, but the dubious honor is available to any character which hasn’t already taken a different Minor Template, as long as they can meet the requirements.
      bBecoming a Goblin:
      Opting into the role of being a Goblin require crafting a black market deal so legendary that become a known as an urban myth for being the devil in the phrase "deal with the Devil". Goblin (which uses the equipment building rules page CoD 100, Resolve + Steetwise if the crafting is done under pressure) and then the Goblin-to-be must enact this deal in a location in the mundane world with a Contrivance Condition matches the deal and is Open or greater. Upon completing the deal to seal the bargain the Goblin-to-be spends a Willpower Dot and immediately loses a Dot of their Stability Advantage. Crafting the story
      The mechanical prerequisites to become a Goblin vary in the following ways
      If the person is a Lost, Hob, or True Fae they require Streetwise 1 and a Skill Specialization Illicit Deals This is because becoming a Goblin is innately intuitive for any of those that are already fae, and the little remaining is the mastery represent in that skill and specialty.If the person is not already fae, they require a Social Merit representing the relationship to the person who is already a Goblin which is instructing the character in how to become a Goblin (even if the Goblin is another Player Character or Support, though this is often done as a Mentor relationship, there is flexibility in which social merit is most appropriate), Streetwise 2 and a Skill Specialization Illicit Deals. Someone who isn’t already a fae and has no relationship to guide them may still stumble across the methods of becoming a Goblin but require Steetwise 4 and a Skill Specialization Illicit Deals.
      Tags Gained: Fae
      Glamorous Will: May spend Glamour instead of Willpower to use Dread Powers, and may spend Willpower instead of Glamour to use Contracts. This does not grant a Glamour Pool, which must be acquired from another source for this to be applicable for the character.
      Potency: The character gains one dot of Potency as if he were a Horror, this affect Willpower Capacity, and Supernatural Tolerance. For those with a Major Template the Supernatural Tolerance does stack with that granted by the Major Template’s Power Stat.
      Hungry to devour hopes and dreams: Every month a Goblin goes without fulfilling their Appetite they lose access to a Willpower Dot, but upon fulfilling the Goblin’s Appetite he regains access to all the Willpower Dots they lost access to, in the state they were lost in (filled with Willpower Points or empty). Goblins also gain Willpower points by fulfilling their Appetite. Unlike Aspirations, Appetite does not grant Beats.
      Appetite: To strictly abide by Bargains the purchaser deeply regrets.
      Dread Powers:
      Goblin Merchant's Eye: A Goblin can see into the depths of its victim’s soul. By spending 1 Willpower, it automatically learns the victim’s Aspirations, telling Personal Aspiration from other types with ease. Also it can keenly see if the subject has previous lost Aspiration through Bargain/Miracles or equivalent powers that trade Aspirations for an effect, knowing what the Aspiration lost was, and what was gained in its place. By spending an additional Willpower point, it learns which current Aspirations if they were lost would cause a breaking point. Subsequent Willpower points reveal the best things to offer for which Aspiration, and in the wording which would seem tempting to the target but could be twisted in intent to cause a breaking point. If the creature uses its knowledge against the victim, it earns an exceptional success on a roll of three successes or more. Accepting a Bargain is always a beat for the victim if it causes a breaking point as intended.(Reworking of Know Soul)
      Goblin Bargain: A Goblin is capable of performing miraculous feats in the name of fulfilling a Goblin Bargain with a Non-Template or an ensoulled Fae being with Personal Aspirations. Such feats include, but aren’t necessarily limited to:
      • Removing (or applying) any Condition or Tilt (or
      similar affliction not modeled by these mechanics).
      • Granting any Merit, Skill, or Attribute at 5 dots.
      • Causing someone to die.
      • Fulfilling an Aspiration of the victim.
      • Grant a Goblin Contract

      The Goblin cannot do any of these things unless explicitly part of deal with with a Non-Template or an ensoulled Fae being who has Personal Aspirations to lose and accepts the Goblin's offer in whose terms include a price crouched in poetic terms symbolic of the Personal Aspiration they will lose in exchanged for a small physical representation of their fulfilled wish, and that deal takes place within a Goblin Stall. Goblins can grant nearly anything but magic of this sort comes with a price. When the Goblin grants a victim’s wish, one of the victim’s Personal Aspirations is destroyed utterly. The character will never be able to fulfill it or replace it with another Aspiration. Use of this power is an irrevocable Atrocity. Any being accepting a Goblin Bargain gains the Be-Careful-What-You-Wish-For Contrivance and 1 Goblin Debt, along with any other Contrivances that fit the nature of the Bargain.
      Goblin Stall: A Goblin can turn a single room, cubical, stall, or tent into a space which is known as a Goblin Stall. It does this by spending 3 Willpower and touching an entrance to the location. The location remains a Goblin Stall for an hour and while it may be entered through the normal means to enter the area, it gains other methods as well. Anyone other than the Goblin who attempts to leave the Goblin Stall must succeed at a Wits + Composure check minus the Goblin’s Resolve each time they attempt to leave the area; if they fail they re-enter the same Goblin Stall, and if they succeed they enter another Goblin Stall within a Mile, the Hedge, or where the exit normally leads (defaulting in this priority if the leaver isn't actively choosing: another Goblin Stall, The Hedge) Additionally Goblin Stalls may be entered at the location corresponding Hedge location. If a Goblin leaves the area the effect ends immediately. The coordinated timing of the use of this dread power that causes interconnection of Goblin Stalls is how Goblin Markets form.

      Storytelling Hints: Goblins often set up a “Goblin Market” with the Maze Dread Power to restrict potential buyers from wandering off, all the while repeatedly offering them deals first persuasively but sometimes through intimidation and even using violence to scare the mortal into buying if the Goblin is desperate enough. Hob Goblins and some Lost Goblins within the Hedge also collectively use these Market as a way to deal with local Changelings to trade Hedge Fruits and make other types of deals, and share the burden of refreshing the Maze when they want to keep customers in.

      Pied-Piper
      Music has many magical qualities, the power to move people, the power to bring hope, crush dreams, comfort those with nothing else, and many more. A Pied Piper is a musician who in a way is also a magician. Their song is their spell and they weave so well. Vapid, catchy, trendy music never has the power to invoke the type of magic used by a Pied-Piper; only melodies that leave listeners haunted by the experience have the kind of power needed. This template predates the Pied-Piper legend, at least as far back as Orpheus.
      Becoming a Pied-Piper:
      Opting into the role of being a Pied-Piper requires great musical skill and a focus on music which touches the heart of those that heart with and lingers like a phantasm in their mind. The Pied-Piper-to-be (which uses the equipment building rules page CoD 100, Composure + Expression if the crafting is done under pressure) must originally compose and play a chosen haunting melody which will become their signature of their Dread Power use in a location in the mundane world with a Magic Music Contrivance Condition and is Open or greater. Upon completing the first playthrough of the composed melody the Pied-Piper-to-be spends a Willpower Dot and immediately loses a Dot of their Stability Advantage as they pour a portion of their soul into the song as they play it.
      The mechanical prerequisites to become a Pied-Piper vary in the following ways
      They standardly require a Social Merit representing the relationship to the person who is already a Pied-Piper which is instructing the character in how to become a Pied-Piper (even if the Pied-Piper is another Player Character or Support, though this is often done as a Mentor relationship, there is flexibility in which social merit is most appropriate), Expression 3 and a Skill Specialization Haunting Music. Someone who has no relationship to guide them may still stumble across the methods of becoming a Pied-Piper but require Expression 4 and a Skill Specialization Haunting Music.
      Tags Gained: Fae
      Glamorous Will: May spend Glamour instead of Willpower to use Dread Powers, and may spend Willpower instead of Glamour to use Contracts. This does not grant a Glamour Pool, which must be acquired from another source for this to be applicable for the character.
      Potency: The character gains one dot of Potency as if he were a Horror, this affect Willpower Capacity, and Supernatural Tolerance. For those with a Major Template the Supernatural Tolerance does stack with that granted by the Major Template’s Power Stat.
      This way to your doom: Every month a Pied-Piper goes without fulfilling their Appetite they lose access to a Willpower Dot, but upon fulfilling the Pied-Piper’s Appetite he regains access to all the Willpower Dots they lost access to, in the state they were lost in (filled with Willpower Points or empty). Fae Courtesan also gain Willpower points by fulfilling their Appetite. Unlike Aspirations, Appetite does not grant Beats.
      Appetite: Use the Follow the Music Dread Power to successful lead an ensoulled being to die or be captured by the True Fae.
      Dread Powers:
      Sooth the Savage Beast: The Pied-Piper's own personally composed haunting melody they created to gain this Minor Template grants them mastery over lesser beasts. By spending 1 Willpower, it can conjure up a swarm of vermin, small animals, fish, or birds as appropriate to its nature. These animals will obey its commands, and it can communicate with them clearly. It can also use this power on a single larger animal.
      Follow the Music: This Dread Power takes the form of the Pied-Piper's own personally composed haunting melody they created to gain this Minor Template, which grants those listening other than the Pied-Piper a vision of a journey and destination the Pied-Piper wishes them to go to, and a magical determination to see it through. The Pied-Piper pays 2 Willpower and rolls Manipulation + Expression. On a success, the subject receives a short vision of the journey and destination the Pied-Piper wishes them to travel, and is under the Obsessed Condition regarding carrying that journey out, as they can't get the melody out of their head until they do.
      Of Music and Madness: The Pied-Piper’s haunting song can also induce madness and terror in its victims. By expending 1 Willpower and making a roll of an Presence + Expression contested by the victim’s Composure, the Horror may inflict one of the following Conditions on the victim: Guilty, Shaken, or Spooked. For 3 Willpower it may instead inflict the Broken, Fugue, or Madness Conditions.


      Mana
      These templates are Mana-based, and associated with the wondrous world of the wizards, where mysteries run deep and profound Truth can make itself know. It is governed by the principle of reaching further than one's grasp into the dangerous unknown, and the consequences of that. Minor Template in this category may purchase Sleepwalker Merits, and opt into part of the Proximi Mechanics (Which comes with the price of accepting the Family Curse), choosing a Family Line that applies to them, and only as follows:
      Originally posted by MtA 2ed pg 307
      Parent Path: Proximi are always linked to one of the five
      Supernal Realms, and except in extremely rare circumstances
      always become members of that Path if they Awaken.
      Blessing Arcana:[/b] Proximi may purchase Blessings from their
      Parent Path’s Ruling Arcana and one other Arcanum...
      Dynastic Blessings: Every Proximus has the capacity to learn
      up to 30 dots worth of Blessings, chosen from the three Blessing
      Arcana at character creation. Blessings may be based on any
      spell of up to three Arcanum dots. Proximi characters buy their
      Blessings from the list for their Dynasty as Merits.
      Limited Casting: Proximi do not have Gnosis or Arcana ratings.
      They use their Willpower as a casting dice pool, penalized
      for spell factors. They may not use Yantras. Their ritual casting
      interval is five hours.
      Reach and Paradox: Proximi gain one risk-free Reach on every Blessing, and gain one die of Paradox risk per extra Reach. A Proximus can’t attempt to contain Paradox like a mage can, but instead of warping the Blessing, any Paradox successes rolled while using a Blessing completely cancel the effect and cause the Proximus’ Curse (see below) to worsen.
      Obvious Blessings with Sleeper witnesses provoke Paradox Risk, trigger Quiescence, and are subject to Dissonance in the same way as Awakened spells.
      Familial Curses: Each family line puts forward not just a talent for magic, but a sometimes-crippling curse that affects a Proximus’ whole life. This is one of the things that theorists point to when saying that Proximi must have been specifically engineered.
      Functionally, a Proximi Curse is a two-part Condition. The first part is a lower-level difficulty that should come up in play, but is not crippling. This stage of the Condition is persistent and cannot be removed even by magic, but generates Beats when it impedes the character. Should the character be forced, by magic or otherwise, to try to escape the curse, the second part of the Proximi Curse kicks in and the character suffers from it until it’s resolved, earning a Beat when the curse reverts to the persistent form.

      Mad Scientist
      Science has promise of wonders but also delivers terror, horrors and revelations mankind was not meant to know. Romantic notions of science being some modern catholicon or panacea to all the world's woes has been completely evicerated by the harsh dystopian consequences that many scientic discovers have brought to societies around the world. None the less there are those with fire in their eyes at the thought of scientific discover no matter the cost. These souls should they pursue their desire through manic devotion and hard work in the proper environment can end up as Mad Scientist.
      Becoming a Mad Scientist:
      Mad Scientist require crafting an object with science that has an Ascentant Condition for their Particular Mad Science Paradigm. The Mad Scientist-to-be (which uses the equipment building rules page CoD 100, Resolve + Science if the crafting is done under pressure) must make this object in a location in the mundane world with a Ascendant Condition suitable to their Particular Mad Science Paradigm and is Open or greater. Upon completing the crafting the Mad Scientist-to-be spends a Willpower Dot and immediately loses a Dot of their Stability Advantage.
      The mechanical prerequisites to become a Mad Scientist vary in the following ways
      If the person is a Mage they require Science 1 and a Skill Specialization in a particular Mad Science Paradigm. This is because becoming a Mad Scientist is innately intuitive for any of those able to bend the Lie to their Supernal will, and the little remaining is the mastery represent in that skill and specialty. If the person is not already a Mage, they require a Social Merit representing the relationship to the person who is already a Mad Scientist which is instructing the character in how to become a Mad Scientist (even if the Mad Scientist is another Player Character or Support, though this is often done as a Mentor relationship, there is flexibility in which social merit is most appropriate), Science 2 and a Skill Specialization in a particular Mad Science Paradigm. Someone who isn’t already a fae and has no relationship to guide them may still stumble across the methods of becoming a Mad Scientist but require Science 4 and a Skill Specialization in a particular Mad Science Paradigm.
      Tags Gained: Erudite
      Willful Mana: May spend Mana instead of Willpower to use Dread Powers, and may spend Willpower instead of Mana when called for in spellcasting. This does not grant a Mana Pool, which must be acquired from another source for this to be applicable for the character.
      Potency: The character gains one dot of Potency as if he were a Horror, this affect Willpower Capacity, and Supernatural Tolerance. For those with a Major Template the Supernatural Tolerance does stack with that granted by the Major Template’s Power Stat.
      For SCIENCE!!!: Every month a Mad Scientist goes without fulfilling their Appetite they lose access to a Willpower Dot, but upon fulfilling the Mad Scientist’s Appetite he regains access to all the Willpower Dots they lost access to, in the state they were lost in (filled with Willpower Points or empty). Mad Scientists also gain Willpower points by fulfilling their Appetite. Unlike Aspirations, Appetite does not grant Beats.
      Appetite: Use Dread Powers over things that are Ascendant with your Mad Scientist Paradigm in a way that causes you to suffer a Breaking Point from doing so and/or to commit an Atrocity.
      Ban: Always have the Obsessed condition about something that counts as an Aspiration.
      Dread Powers:
      Strengthen via Mad Science: The Mad Scientist can with a Intelligence + Science roll enhance objects, forces, and creature that have the Ascendant Condition associated with the particular type of Mad Science they practice. It can add to the Defense of an experiment subject, make an emotion stronger, an animal or plant healthier, or an object more robust, gaining the Mad Scientist’s Potency + any other Supernatural Tolerance in Health or Structure. This Dread Power can shift the Ascendant Condition associated with the particular type of Mad Science to Open for its duration. The cost is 1 Willpower and the Duration is 10 minutes per success.
      Manipulate via Mad Science: The Mad Scientist can make minor changes to objects, forces, and creature that have the Ascendant Condition associated with the particular type of Mad Science they practice, such as slightly changing the nature or target of an emotion, or make minor changes to an animal’s
      actions, a plant’s growth, or an object’s functionality. The cost is 2 Willpower and the Duration is an hour per success.
      Control via Mad Science: The Mad Scientist can make dramatic changes within objects, forces, and creature that have the Ascendant Condition associated with the particular type of Mad Science, twisting emotions entirely or dictating an animal’s actions, a plant’s growth or an object’s functionality. This Influence can shift the Open Condition to Controlled for its duration. The cost is 3 Willpower and the Duration is a day per success.

      Miracle-Worker
      Faith is miraculous in a world so dark and filled with terrors. It is a divine spark which shines brightly against the dying of the light in the rest of creation. There are many different religious faiths and among those faiths there are those that studiously examine their beliefs to perfect them and have the ability to harness the collective metaphysical power of the faith behind the religious for the benefit of those that truly believe. These are Miracle-Workers.
      Becoming a Miracle-Worker:
      Miracle-Worker require dedicated ongoing intercessory prayer for a non-template whom has an Ascendant Condition for their Particular Religious Paradigm. The Miracle-Worker-to-be (which uses the equipment building rules page CoD 100, Resolve + Academics if the crafting is done under pressure) must make this prayer in a location in the mundane world with a Ascendant Condition suitable to their Particular Religious Paradigm and is Open or greater. Upon completing the this prayer for a number of days significant to the faith the Miracle-Worker-to-be spends a Willpower Dot and immediately loses a Dot of their Stability Advantage as a sign of their single minded devotion to their faith.
      The mechanical prerequisites to become a Miracle-Worker vary in the following ways
      If the person is a Mage they require Academics 1 and a Skill Specialization in a particular Religious Paradigm. This is because becoming a Miracle-Worker is innately intuitive for any of those able to bend the Lie to their Supernal will, and the little remaining is the mastery represent in that skill and specialty. If the person is not already a Mage, they require a Social Merit representing the relationship to the person who is already a Miracle-Worker which is instructing the character in how to become a Miracle-Worker (even if the Miracle-Worker is another Player Character or Support, though this is often done as a Mentor relationship, there is flexibility in which social merit is most appropriate), Science 2 and a Skill Specialization in a particular Religious Paradigm. Someone who isn’t already a Mage and has no relationship to guide them may still stumble across the methods of becoming a Miracle-Worker but require Academics 4 and a Skill Specialization in a particular Religious Paradigm.
      Tags Gained: Faithful
      Willful Mana: May spend Mana instead of Willpower to use Dread Powers, and may spend Willpower instead of Mana when called for in spellcasting. This does not grant a Mana Pool, which must be acquired from another source for this to be applicable for the character.
      Potency: The character gains one dot of Potency as if he were a Horror, this affect Willpower Capacity, and Supernatural Tolerance. For those with a Major Template the Supernatural Tolerance does stack with that granted by the Major Template’s Power Stat.
      Piety Refreshes the Soul: Every month a Miracle-Worker goes without fulfilling their Appetite they lose access to a Willpower Dot, but upon fulfilling the Miracle-Worker’s Appetite he regains access to all the Willpower Dots they lost access to, in the state they were lost in (filled with Willpower Points or empty). Miracle-Workers also gain Willpower points by fulfilling their Appetite. Unlike Aspirations, Appetite does not grant Beats.
      Appetite: Maintaining an Open Ascendant Condition for your particular Religious Paradigm in a site of worship for Non-Template followers of you Religious Paradigm for at least one ceremony of that faith.
      Bane: Committing an Atrocity against someone who has Ascendant Condition for your particular Religious Paradigm.
      Dread Powers:
      Strengthen via Faith: The Miracle-Worker can with a momentary benediction and a Intelligence + Academics roll enhance objects, forces, and creature that have the Ascendant Condition associated with the particular type of Religious Paradigm they practice. He can add to the Defense of an parishioner, make an emotion stronger, an animal or plant healthier, or an object more robust, gaining the Mad Scientist’s Potency + any other Supernatural Tolerance in Health or Structure. This Dread Power can shift the Ascendant Condition associated with the particular type of Religious Paradigm to Open for its duration. The cost is 1 Willpower and the Duration is 10 minutes per success.
      Miracle: A Miracle-Worker, as the name implies, is capable of performing miraculous feats by praying for a Non-Template which has the Open Ascendant Condition for their particular to Religious Paradigm if they have remaining Personal Aspirations. Such feats include, but aren’t necessarily limited to:
      • Removing (or applying) any Condition or Tilt (or
      similar affliction not modeled by these mechanics).
      • Granting any Merit, Skill, or Attribute at 5 dots.
      • Causing someone to die.
      • Fulfilling an Aspiration of the victim.

      The Miracle-Worker cannot do any of these things unless explicitly requested to pray for it by a Non-Template which has the Open Ascendant Condition for their Particular to Religious Paradigm who has Personal Aspirations to lose. When the Miracle-Worker grants a victim’s wish through the power of their Prayer, one of the victim’s Personal Aspirations is destroyed utterly. The character will never be able to fulfill it or replace it with another Aspiration. Use of this power is an irrevocable Atrocity if done in a way that isn't in sincere compassionate service to Non-Template who makes the request. The Non-Template feels the loss of desire represented by the granting of their miracle, and it is common to attribute this to a more content heart.
      Sense Sin: The Miracle-Worker can sense if others they are speaking with measure up to their faith. By spending 1 Willpower, he automatically learns the victim’s Virtue, Vice, Aspirations, and current Stability Advantage. By spending an additional Willpower point, he learns the circumstances of the character’s most recent Atrocity. Subsequent Willpower points reveal older Atrocities. If the Miracle-Worker uses his knowledge against the victim, it earns an exceptional success on a roll of three successes or more.


      Monk
      The juxtaposition of a martial artist who is dedicated to non-violence created a dynamic that is metaphysically fueled. Tapping into the paradox deepens the two extremes of capacity for and deliberate eschewing of violence. A Monk has very little to worry about from most brawlers, or even those fulling armed, and could inflict terrible damage to them, but as a general rule doesn't. A Monk became so through a severe testing of their skill and conviction and are often seen as someone of extreme integrity and competence.
      Becoming a Monk:
      A Monk requires seeking to transcend violence while deeply studying fighting forms which make it easier. The Monk-to-be (which uses the equipment building rules page CoD 100, Resolve + Brawl if the crafting is done under pressure in downtime) must withstand a violent confrontation with an Ensoulled being who fully intends to kill the Monk-to-be without the Monk-to-be inflicting harm to an Ensoulled being, and without fleeing. After the end of the confrontation the Monk-to-be spends a Willpower Dot to dedicate themselves pacifism and immediately loses a Dot of their Stability Advantage as their devotion consumes a little bit of their ability to form attachments.
      The mechanical prerequisites to become a Monk vary in the following ways
      They standardly require a Social Merit representing the relationship to the person who is already a Monk which is instructing the character in how to become a Monk (even if the Monk is another Player Character or Support, though this is often done as a Mentor relationship, there is flexibility in which social merit is most appropriate), Brawl 3 and a Skill Specialization Pacifism. Someone who has no relationship to guide them may still stumble across the methods of becoming a Monk but require Brawl 4 and a Skill Specialization Pacifism.
      Tags Gained: Faithful
