      Willful Mana: May spend Mana instead of Willpower to use Dread Powers, and may spend Willpower instead of Mana when called for in spellcasting. This does not grant a Mana Pool, which must be acquired from another source for this to be applicable for the character.
      Potency: The character gains one dot of Potency as if he were a Horror, this affect Willpower Capacity, and Supernatural Tolerance. For those with a Major Template the Supernatural Tolerance does stack with that granted by the Major Template’s Power Stat.
      Longing for Serenity: Every month a Monk goes without fulfilling their Appetite they lose access to a Willpower Dot, but upon fulfilling the Monk’s Appetite he regains access to all the Willpower Dots they lost access to, in the state they were lost in (filled with Willpower Points or empty). Monks also gain Willpower points by fulfilling their Appetite. Unlike Aspirations, Appetite does not grant Beats.
      Appetite: Going a month without engaging in combat.
      Bane: Damaging another Ensoulled being.
      Dread Powers:
      Adamantine Form: The Monk is nearly indestructible. Any attack that does not score an exceptional success inflicts only a single point of bashing damage. Exceptional successes inflict damage as normal. If the Monk has a weakness of some sort, attacks incorporating that weakness bypass this power.
      Distant Strike: The Monk may wound opponents and break objects at a distance by sending fist sized shockwaves of force towards them. Range is equal to 10 yards per dot of Resolve, and the Monk does not suffer range penalties. If the Resolve + Brawl activation roll succeeds, the Blast wounds as a lethal weapon. The Monk may increase the lethality of his Distant Strike by paying Willpower — every 2 Willpower spent increases the “weapon” by one lethal damage. The maximum weapon bonus is equal to the Monk’s Potency + any other Supernatural Tolerance.
      Rippleless Pond: The Monk sends out a wave of powerful calm. This Dread Power costs 1 Willpower, and lasts for a scene, or until the Monk uses another takes an offensive action. The activation roll of Presence + Empathy is made once, but anyone coming within five yards of the Monk must make a Resolve + Composure + Supernatural Tolerance roll. If the activation roll had more successes, the victim suffers a -2 dice penalty to all violent or confrontational actions as long as the aura remains. If the victim gains more successes, he is immune to the aura unless the Monk uses the Dread Power again.

      Tremere Reaper
      The existence of the Tremere Reaper casts a dark shadow over the presumption of innate goodness of sapient beings. Few things are as awful as the devouring of souls, and yet that is exactly what Tremere Reapers do. Souls are immortal and thus can be used to defy death, both of the Tremere and those they care about and the fact these aspects of creation were meant to be everlasting doesn't cause them to stop from snuffing them out for the power over life and death. Reviled by all those that know what they do, the Tremere Reapers still are quite powerful because replenishing their numbers after even well planned attacks them is as easy as the survivors eating some souls to bring back their fallen comrades.
      Becoming a Tremere Reaper:
      An Tremere Reaper requires studing the medical arts with the drive to find the secret of immortality at any cost. Acquiring their first soul for this Template is always the hardest. The Tremere Reaper-to-be (which uses the equipment building rules page CoD 100, Resolve + Medicine if the crafting is done under pressure in downtime) must vivisect an Non-Template with a soul and extract it. This process can only be done once for that Tremere Reaper-to-be as part of their transformation. The process leaves the victim with the Soulless Condition, if the Tremere Reaper allows them to survive, at the climax of the Procedure the Tremere Reaper-to-be spends a Willpower Dot and immediately loses a Dot of their Stability Advantage as he devours his own soul and replaces it with that of his victim.
      The mechanical prerequisites to become a Tremere Reaper vary in the following ways
      They standardly require a Social Merit representing the relationship to the person who is already a Tremere Reaper which is instructing the character in how to become a Tremere Reaper (even if the Tremere Reaper is another Player Character or Support, though this is often done as a Mentor relationship, there is flexibility in which social merit is most appropriate), Medicine 3 and a Skill Specialization Immortality. Someone who has no relationship to guide them may still stumble across the methods of becoming a Tremere Reaper but require Medicine 4 and a Skill Specialization Immortality.
      Tags Gained: Erudite, Vampire
      Willful Mana: May spend Mana instead of Willpower to use Dread Powers, and may spend Willpower instead of Mana when called for in spellcasting. This does not grant a Mana Pool, which must be acquired from another source for this to be applicable for the character.
      Potency: The character gains one dot of Potency as if he were a Horror, this affect Willpower Capacity, and Supernatural Tolerance. For those with a Major Template the Supernatural Tolerance does stack with that granted by the Major Template’s Power Stat.
      Hollow Hunger: Every month a Tremere Reaper goes without fulfilling their Appetite they lose access to a Willpower Dot, but upon fulfilling the Tremere Reaper’s Appetite he regains access to all the Willpower Dots they lost access to, in the state they were lost in (filled with Willpower Points or empty). Tremere Reapers also gain Willpower points by fulfilling their Appetite. Unlike Aspirations, Appetite does not grant Beats.
      Appetite: Dissolving a soul for nourishment.
      Bane: Not fulfilling Appetite to the point that invokes Hollow Hunger's loss of access to Willpower Dots.
      Dread Powers:
      Immortal: The Tremere Reaper isn’t bound by mortal laws of life and death. He’s not indestructible, per se, but if he’s killed by anything other than one of his Banes, he just comes back in a later scene like nothing happened. Even if he’s destroyed with one of his Banes, he’s never really gone — another Tremere Reaper that knows of them and their death can consume a soul they have for the purpose of invoking the dead Tremere Reaper's Immortal resurrection clause, which causes the dead Tremere Reaper to reform alive and well as if they never died in the presence of the one resurrecting them.
      Soul-Eater: The Tremere Reaper is capable of stealing human souls for sustenance and to use via the Essential Salts Dread Power or any other ability to harness a soul he may have. He has to trick the victim into offering it up willingly without Hard Leverage, then the Tremere Reaper may spend 3 Willpower and roll Intelligence + Medicine, contested by the victim’s Resolve. If the Tremere Reaper succeeds, it steals the victim’s soul and inflicts the Soulless Condition. The victim (or her friends) may be able to find a way to restore her soul by killing the Tremere Reaper with any Bane he may have, if the soul is still in possession of the Tremere Reaper. The Tremere Reaper may hold as many souls as he has in Potency + any other Supernatural Tolerance + their own soul and may consume any specific one as an Instant Action to fulfill his Appetite, or to invoke the Resurrection clause of any dead Tremere they know of (both of which annihilates the soul in question). Using his own soul leaves the Tremere Reaper with the Soulless Condition. He may move any soul he is holding to where his soul should be to resolve this Condition as a Reflexive action. This grants no Beats.
      Essential Salts: This Dread Power literally raises the dead. The Dread Power costs 10 Souls to use, the activation roll of Intelligence + Medicine with access to the remains of the deceased which is reduced to its essential salts, and is penalized by one die per week the subject has been dead. Supernatural beings that have already died as part of their transformation — vampires, mummies, and Sin-Eaters — can’t be resurrected, nor can anyone who died of natural causes. Other supernatural beings lose their powers when resurrected, becoming a Non-Template. Additionally those brought back have the Soulless Condition.


      Plasm
      These templates are Plasm-based, and associated with the dark brooding world of the dead, where . It is governed by the principles of the travel towards oblivion, and the dissolution of emotion and identity. Minor Template in this category may purchase POWER-TBD, and qualify to buy any Merits which are Bound Specific.


      Body Thief
      Wanting to be someone else is something that is normal to experience in developmental stages as one's identity forms, but there are times when that identity formation is arrested leaving a gap aching to be filled, the pain of self hatred causes the idea of having someone else's identity as an escape, and finally mere covetousness that exceeds more than what a person has to whom the person is in a perverse desire to take something not meant to be taken. All these things are paths that can lead to becoming a Body Thief.
      Becoming a Body Thief
      Becoming a Body Thief requires radical coveting another person’s life or identity to the point that it becomes a white hot burning insanity. It always includes the potential Body Thief having an Obsession Condition about emulating, stalking, or becoming like their first target. A Body Thief’s first victim is always someone who already has the Soulless condition, sometimes this is a coincidence, other times this is arranged. While all the other Minor Templates may be entered into without understanding the full implications, Body Thief is the most likely to be accidentally joined. The Obsession changes into a power when the correct constellation of factors align. Body-Thief-to-be will feel a profound moment of connection to their first target, and they have an opportunity to spend a Willpower Dot to “hold on tighter”, “push the intimacy further”, or “explore the connection more deeply”. If they do they they must decide what type of connection it is and they gain this Minor Template.
      The mechanical prerequisites to become a Body Thief vary in the following ways
      They standardly require a Social Merit representing the relationship to the person who is already a Body-Thief which is instructing the character in how to become a Body-Thief (even if the Body-Thief is another Player Character or Support, though this is often done as a Mentor relationship, there is flexibility in which social merit is most appropriate), Larceny 3 and a Skill Specialization Personal Affects. Someone who has no relationship to guide them may still stumble across the methods of becoming a Body-Thief but require Larceny 4 and a Skill Specialization Personal Affects.
      Tags Gained: Thought-Form
      Grave Will: May spend Plasm instead of Willpower to use Dread Powers, and may spend Willpower instead of other Plasm when using a Haunt. This does not grant an Plasm Pool, which must be acquired from another source for this to be applicable for the character.
      Potency: The character gains one dot of Potency as if he were a Horror, this affect Willpower Capacity, and Supernatural Tolerance. For those with a Major Template the Supernatural Tolerance does stack with that granted by the Major Template’s Power Stat.
      Nourished by Souls: Every month a Body Thief goes without fulfilling their Appetite she loses access to a Willpower Dot, but upon fulfilling the Body Thief’s Appetite she regains access to all the Willpower Dots she lost access to, in the state they were lost in(filled with Willpower Points or empty). Skinchanger also gain Willpower points by fulfilling her Appetite. Unlike Aspirations, Appetite does not grant Beats.
      Appetite: Use the Soul Thief Dread power on a new victim to devour the soul.
      Dread Powers:
      Soul Thief: Upon gaining this Minor Template the new Body Thief must immediately decide between Mind, Body, or Soul, as the most important. Depending on their choice it affect the restriction under which they may use their Soul Thief Dread Power. Once the restriction is met, the creature may spend 3 Willpower and roll an appropriate dice pool, contested by the victim’s Resolve. If the creature succeeds, it steals the victim’s soul and inflicts the Soulless Condition. The soul is immediately devoured upon using this power, fulfilling the Body Thief's Appetite, and the victim will not be able to find a way to restore her own soul, but other Souls are capable of Resolving the Soulless Condition.
      Mind: To Use the Soul Thief Dread Power upon someone else they must be an ensoulled being without the Soulless Condition you must convince them to willingly accept giving you complete power over their life, which they willingly seal by them spending a Willpower Dot.
      Body: To Use the Soul Thief Dread Power upon someone else they must be an ensoulled being without the soulless Condition you personally must render them susceptible to a Killing Blow from you. Having others make them susceptible to your killing blow lacks the intimacy required for the power to work.
      Soul: To Use the Soul Thief Dread Power upon someone else they must be an ensoulled being without the Soulless Condition you must coerce them to commit an Atrocity.
      Hollow-Out Soulless: The Body Thief can with a Presence + Larceny roll Power can shift the Soulless Condition to count as if it was any Base Ephemeral Condition (Anchored, Resonant, ect...) that is Open for its duration, allowing all Manifestations that require such an ability on the person with the Soulless Condition. The cost is 1 Willpower and the Duration is 10 minutes per success.
      Host Jump The Body Thief counts as Claiming the body it currently resides in and may leap from host to host when using Claim Manifestations. The current host must touch and steal a Personal Affects of the intended host while the Body Thief spends 3 Willpower, and the new host must have a Soulless Condition which currently affected by the Hollow-Out Soulless Dread Power. If both prerequisites are met, the Body Thief immediately transfers the Claimed Condition to the new host, although Claimed must begin the process of Claiming again. The Body Thief does not need to re-spend Willpower on the Manifestation Effect when jumping hosts with this Dread Power. Each new Claim victim Claim body vacated by the Body Thief as this power happens to them instead of suffering the after effects listed under the Claimed Condition. Treat the victim and Body Thief both as having zero Power, Finesse, and Resistance for changing attributes. Note all Templates, Permanent Atrocity, Conditions about the Soul, Supports, and Slasher Status follow the identity, though this will cause a basic 3 die Stress roll upon first interacting with each particular Support after the change, and may cause the Support to become aware of the Supernatural for Aggravated Hurt.
      If a Claim makes the trade more or less permanent, access to Merits of the body Jumped into is managed via Sanctity of Merits, meaning total number of Merits remain in equilibrium to what the Claiming character has purchased. This means an inexperienced Body Thief which isn't invested in managing Merits can lose access to many of the Merits of a host that was heavily invested into managing Merits, while a Body Thief that was more heavily invested in managing Merits can maintain everything their host has and possibly grow or retain mental based merits they previously had. It is basically an opportunity to arrange the Merits as best to reflect the exchange of bodies)


      Ferryman
      Transporting the dead to the afterlife is an ancient, and potentially profitable, calling. Those with the proper temperament are uncommon, while those with the actual knack are even more rare. Oral traditions older than the written language tell tales about those who traffic with the dead and lead them to the underworld.
      Becoming a Ferryman:
      Ferryman requires a transporting a ghost to a destination which has the Menace Condition. The Ferryman-to-be (which uses the equipment building rules page CoD 100, Resolve + Drive if the crafting is done under pressure) must end this journey with the Ghost in a location in the mundane world with a Menace Condition suitable to the ghost's death Threshold. Upon completing the this journey the Ferryman-to-be spends a Willpower Dot and immediately loses a Dot of their Stability Advantage which also opens the Avernian Gate associated Menace Condition of the location, pulling the ghost in as if it has no Anchors.
      The mechanical prerequisites to become a Ferryman vary in the following ways
      If the person is a Sin-Eater they require Drive 1 and a Skill Specialization Crossroads. This is because becoming a Ferryman is innately intuitive for any of those linked to a Geist, and the little remaining is the mastery represent in that skill and specialty. If the person is not already a Sin-Eater, they require a Social Merit representing the relationship to the person who is already a Ferryman which is instructing the character in how to become a Ferryman (even if the Ferryman is another Player Character or Support, though this is often done as a Mentor relationship, there is flexibility in which social merit is most appropriate), Drive 2 and a Skill Specialization Crossroads. Someone who isn’t already a Sin-Eater and has no relationship to guide them may still stumble across the methods of becoming a Ferryman but require Drive 4 and a Skill Specialization Crossroads.
      Tags Gained:
      Willful Plasm: May spend Plasm instead of Willpower to use Dread Powers, and may spend Willpower instead of Plasm when called for POWERS. This does not grant a Plasm Pool, which must be acquired from another source for this to be applicable for the character.
      Potency: The character gains one dot of Potency as if he were a Horror, this affect Willpower Capacity, and Supernatural Tolerance. For those with a Major Template the Supernatural Tolerance does stack with that granted by the Major Template’s Power Stat.
      Psychopomp's Duty: Every month a Ferryman goes without fulfilling their Appetite they lose access to a Willpower Dot, but upon fulfilling the Ferryman’s Appetite he regains access to all the Willpower Dots they lost access to, in the state they were lost in (filled with Willpower Points or empty). Ferrymen also gain Willpower points by fulfilling their Appetite. Unlike Aspirations, Appetite does not grant Beats.
      Appetite: Guide a ghost one river deeper into the Underworld
      Bane: Guiding a ghost across an Underworld river towards the material world.
      Dread Powers:
      Open the Hungry Gate: This Dread Power has two aspects, the first being for those who are not bound to a Geist and the second for those that are. The Non-SinEater Ferryman can with a Resolve + Drive roll can shift the Menace Condition of an Avernian Gate to Open and makes them passable for that period. The cost is 1 Willpower and the Duration is 10 minutes per success. The use of this Dread Power exclusive to Sin-Eaters costs 10 Willpower to use, the activation roll of Intelligence + Drive with access to the ghost of the deceased in the Underworld, and is penalized by one die per week the subject has been dead. Supernatural beings that have already died as part of their transformation — vampires, mummies, and Sin-Eaters — can’t be resurrected, nor can anyone who died of natural causes. Other supernatural beings lose their powers when resurrected, becoming a Non-Template. Additionally those brought back have the Soulless Condition.
      Underworld Labyrinth: A Ferryman can turn an confined section of the any underground structure in the material world into a space which is known as a Underworld Labyrinth. He does this by spending 3 Willpower and touching a wall, floor or ceiling in the location. The location remains a Underworld Labyrinth for an hour, and during that time the space no longer has a state of Twilight as if they everything there was already in the Underworld and it may be entered through the normal means to enter the area. Anyone other than the Ferryman who attempts to leave the Underworld Labyrinth must succeed at a Wits + Composure check minus the Ferryman’s Resolve each time they attempt to leave the area; if they fail they enter the Underworld equivalent of the location, and if they succeed they can leave where the exit normally leads. The Ferryman may use this to enter the Underworld when he likes. If a Ferryman leaves the area the effect ends immediately.
      Death's Route: This Dread Power allows an Ferryman to know the quickest route to a destination. The fastest route isn’t always the safest, and the Dread Power doesn’t reveal any dangers on the way, only a set of directions to the target. If the destination is the subject of the Safe Place Merit, the activation roll is contested by the lowest Resolve + Supernatural Tolerance among any owners. The Dread Power costs 1 Willpower, and lasts for a scene. If the destination is too far away to reach that quickly, the entity must use the Dread Power again.

      Pallbearer
      It is frequently said that people morning the dead should let go and move on with their lives, that death is a natural part of life and staying overly attached to the dead is unhealthy. What the cynical of the world won't admit but even they know in their heart is that letting the dead be attached to you is even more unhealthy. Some people have a knack and innate itch for unburdening those with ephemeral undead baggage, and the most adept of these are Pallbearers.
      Becoming a Pallbearer:
      Pallbearer requires opening allow the Doors of a person grieving the death of a ghost which is Anchored to them, in order to convince them to "let go" of that person and move one with their life. The Pallbearer-to-be (which uses the equipment building rules page CoD 100, Resolve + Drive if the crafting is done under pressure) must then succeed an abjuration check to remove the Anchored Condition after they have succeeded opening the Doors. Upon completing the this task the Pallbearer-to-be spends a Willpower Dot and immediately loses a Dot of their Stability Advantage, as a toll for gaining the ability to affect the dead's connection upon the living.
      The mechanical prerequisites to become a Pallbearer vary in the following ways
      If the person is a Sin-Eater they require Empathy 1 and a Skill Specialization The Grieving. This is because becoming a Pallbearer is innately intuitive for any of those linked to a Geist, and the little remaining is the mastery represent in that skill and specialty. If the person is not already a Sin-Eater, they require a Social Merit representing the relationship to the person who is already a Pallbearer which is instructing the character in how to become a Pallbearer (even if the Pallbearer is another Player Character or Support, though this is often done as a Mentor relationship, there is flexibility in which social merit is most appropriate), Empathy 2 and a Skill Specialization The Grieving. Someone who isn’t already a Sin-Eater and has no relationship to guide them may still stumble across the methods of becoming a Pallbearer but require Empathy 4 and a Skill Specialization The Grieving.
      Tags Gained:
      Willful Plasm: May spend Plasm instead of Willpower to use Dread Powers, and may spend Willpower instead of Plasm when called for POWERS. This does not grant a Plasm Pool, which must be acquired from another source for this to be applicable for the character.
      Potency: The character gains one dot of Potency as if he were a Horror, this affect Willpower Capacity, and Supernatural Tolerance. For those with a Major Template the Supernatural Tolerance does stack with that granted by the Major Template’s Power Stat.
      Compulsive Consoler: Every month a Pallbearer goes without fulfilling their Appetite they lose access to a Willpower Dot, but upon fulfilling the Pallbearer’s Appetite he regains access to all the Willpower Dots they lost access to, in the state they were lost in (filled with Willpower Points or empty). Pallbearer also gain Willpower points by fulfilling their Appetite. Unlike Aspirations, Appetite does not grant Beats.
      Appetite: Help someone who is grieving move on from the ghost Anchored to them by Opening their Doors to convince them to and then successfully using abjuration to remove the Anchored Condition.
      Dread Powers:
      Aura of Grief: The Pallbearer sends out a wave of powerful — and distracting — grief. This Dread Power costs 1 Essence, and lasts for a scene, or until the Pallbearer uses another Numina. The activation roll is made once, but anyone coming within five yards of the Pallbearer must make a Resolve + Composure + Supernatural Tolerance roll. If the activation roll had more successes, the victim suffers a -2 dice penalty to all actions as long as the aura remains. If the victim gains more successes, he is immune to the aura unless the Pallbearer uses the Dread Power again.
      Strengthen Anchor: The Mad Scientist can with a Presence + Empathy roll enhance objects, forces, and creature that have the Anchored Condition. It can add to the Defense of a subject against the ghost whose Anchored Condition it is, make the subject's grief stronger, an animal or plant healthier, or an object more robust, gaining the Pallbearer’s Potency + any other Supernatural Tolerance in Health or Structure. This Dread Power can shift the Anchored Condition to Open for its duration. The cost is 1 Willpower and the Duration is 10 minutes per success.
      Manipulate Anchor: The Pallbearer can make minor changes to objects, forces, and creature that have the Anchored Condition, such as slightly changing the target of the subject's grief, or make minor changes to an animal’s actions, a plant’s growth, or an object’s functionality. The cost is 2 Willpower and the Duration is an hour per success.

      Thuggee
      Some view death with a mindless fear which robs them of reason to the point they can't contemplate the topic in any meaningful way, others take it as a natural part of the cycle of existence, and yet others see it not as something profanely frightful nor even banally normal but as something that is preternaturally sacred. Those that become Thuggee are always of this third group. Death has the power to make the world a better place, it can be sweet and merciful, and it can be righteous and just. Thuggee deal in these types of death, and more importantly the type of death they deal is more final and absolute cessation of existence than mere mundane death, this sacred murder allows them to give life to those they deem worthy.
      Becoming a Thuggee:
      A Thuggee requires a scarf they have devoutly prayed over for the strength to end a wicked person who has committed Atrocities and has no more than 1 Stability Advantage, whom they will murder with said scarf. The Thuggee-to-be (which uses the equipment building rules page CoD 100, Resolve + Weaponry if the crafting is done under pressure in downtime) must kill the person using the scarf to strangle and inflict the damage. After the end of the confrontation the Thuggee-to-be spends a Willpower Dot to dedicate themselves the ending of that person's life and immediately loses a Dot of their Stability Advantage as their devotion consumes a little bit of their ability to form attachments.
      The mechanical prerequisites to become a Thuggee vary in the following ways
      They standardly require a Social Merit representing the relationship to the person who is already a Thuggee which is instructing the character in how to become a Thuggee (even if the Thuggee is another Player Character or Support, though this is often done as a Mentor relationship, there is flexibility in which social merit is most appropriate), Weaponry 3 and a Skill Specialization Silk Scarves. Someone who has no relationship to guide them may still stumble across the methods of becoming a Thuggee but require Weaponry 4 and a Skill Specialization Silk Scarves.
      Tags Gained: Faithful
      Willful Plasm: May spend Plasm instead of Willpower to use Dread Powers, and may spend Willpower instead of Plasm when called for in POWERS. This does not grant a Plasm Pool, which must be acquired from another source for this to be applicable for the character.
      Potency: The character gains one dot of Potency as if he were a Horror, this affect Willpower Capacity, and Supernatural Tolerance. For those with a Major Template the Supernatural Tolerance does stack with that granted by the Major Template’s Power Stat.
      Hand of Judgment: Every month a Thuggee goes without fulfilling their Appetite they lose access to a Willpower Dot, but upon fulfilling the Thuggee’s Appetite he regains access to all the Willpower Dots they lost access to, in the state they were lost in (filled with Willpower Points or empty). Thuggee also gain Willpower points by fulfilling their Appetite. Unlike Aspirations, Appetite does not grant Beats.
      Appetite: Use the Sacred Murder Dread Power
      Bane: Dying after becoming a Thuggee and coming back from the dead.
      Dread Powers:
      Judge Worth: The Thuggee can sense if others they can observe are moral. By spending 1 Willpower, he automatically learns the victim’s Virtue, Vice, Aspirations, Permanent Atrocity and current Stability Advantage and Temporary Atrocity. By spending an additional Willpower point, he learns the circumstances of the character’s most recent Atrocity. Subsequent Willpower points reveal older Atrocities. If the Thuggee uses his knowledge against the victim, it earns an exceptional success on a roll of three successes or more. By use of this Dread Power on the records about a subject, a Thuggee can tell if they are alive or dead, and if dead if they qualify for Resurrection of the Righteous Dread Power.
      Sacred Murder: By having a scarf wrapped around the throat of a Slasher, or Ensouled being with only 1 Stability Advantage and Atrocity of either type, which in combat requires an Grapple overpower maneuver to place, the Thuggee may spend 3 Willpower and roll a Weaponry + Strength roll contested by the victim’s Resolve. If the thuggee succeeds, it steals the victim’s soul and seals its fate, inflicting the Soulless and Obliterated Condition. The soul is contained in the scarf, and may be used by any being capable of using Souls by handling the scarf. The victim may reclaim their Soul by destroying the scarf which contains their specific Soul but this does not affect the Obliterated Condition.
      Resurrection of the Righteous: This Dread Power literally raises the dead. The Dread Power costs 7 Souls to use, and may be used from any object which holds a Soul. It Dread Power only works on those which had no permanent Atrocity, had a Stability Advantage of 8 or higher, and had the Virtuous Merit. The activation roll of Composure + Weaponry as the items are gently draped over a still born infant less than a day old. As the process is successful the infant comes alive and physically transforms into the person being resurrected as they were when they were 7 years old, Gaining the Physically a Child, and Social a Child Persistent Conditions, but remembers everything before their death and only gain Mentally a Child Condition if they also had that at the time of their Death (because of dementia, actually being child, or ect...). Supernatural beings lose their powers when resurrected, becoming a Non-Template.

      Storyteller's note: Please consider many famous long dead Virtuous people are likely alive and well, already brought by by a Thuggee, as the availability of greater and lesser Slashers for them to hunt is at an all time high,

      Vitae
      These templates are Vitae-based, and associated with the damned world of the vampires, where there is no one you can trust because you're just a resource to be exploited. It is governed by the principles of blood sorcery, dangerous byzantine politics, and eternal rivalries. Minor Template in this category may purchase Common Disciplines at Out of Clan prices, and qualify to buy any Merits which are Ghoul Specific. This also applies to Vampire Specific Merits at ST discretion.

      Bloodbather
      There is life and mystical power in blood, and this is something any vampire could tell you, and most could tell you that with study that life and mystical power can be tapped into. The practice of Bloodbathing is one of the most common ways to tap into that power, and have benefits that entice even Kindred to practice this art.
      Becoming a Bloodbather:
      Becoming Bloodbather requires devising a bloodbathing ritual, which is described below. The process of devising a bloodbathing ritual (which uses the equipment building rules page CoD 100, Resolve + Occult if the crafting is done under pressure). Upon completing the ritual, the Bloodbather-to-be spends a Willpower Dot and immediately loses a Dot of their Stability Advantage to bind their life force to the ritual.
      The mechanical prerequisites to become a Bloodbather vary in the following ways
      If the character already has the Vampire Tag, they require Occult 1 and a Skill Specialization related to blood magic or human sacrifice. This is because becoming a Bloodbather is innately intuitive for any of those that are already sustained via vampirism, and the little remaining is the mastery represented in that skill and specialty.
      If the person is not already a vampire, they require a Social Merit representing the relationship to the person who is already a Bloodbather which is instructing the character in how to become an Bloodbather (even if the Bloodbather is another Player Character or Support, though this is often done as a Mentor relationship, there is flexibility in which social merit is most appropriate), Occult 2 and a Skill Specialization related to blood magic.
      Someone who isn’t already a vampire and has no relationship to guide them may still stumble across the methods of becoming an Bloodbather but require Occult 4 and a Skill Specialization related to blood magic.
      Tags Gained: Vampire
      More than one way to bleed:
      There are fundamentally different variants of the Bloodbathing ritual and they are as follows:
      Ghoul Ritual: Another character with the Vampire Tag anoints or baths the Bloodbather with portion of their blood and power (Equal to an a Vitae for a Kindred or Strix, a Willpower for another Bloodbather or Horror which also counts as a vampire). The vampire character using their blood to anoint the Bloodbather to fulfilling the Bloodbather’s Appetite is the holder of a Ghouled Peristent Condition over the character whose Appetite they Fulfilled. (Please note the Bloodbather may not be forced to fulfill their Appetite against their will. To accept a vampire providing their power to fulfill the Bloodbather’s Appetite is to accept that vampire as a master.)
      Atrocious Ritual: The bloodbathing ritual is described in terms that make it a 2-4 point Atrocity which involve bathing in blood and is committed by the Bloodbather. The Atrocity must be done is a ceremonial way, and its details must be consistent with how it was first described when this minor template was gained.
      Cult Ritual: This ritual requires being a top ranking member of a Mystery Cult Initiation (Which means having the 5th dot) whose ultimate secret purpose is to maintain those at top as Bloodbathers through either committing atrocities for the leaders or through sharing the burden of bloodletting among willing devotees. Which way the cults work is defined when founding the Mystery Cult, but either way maintaining the Bloodbather template is the benefit not expressed game mechanic granted by the 5th dot of this particular Mystery Cult. The number of times the cult can fulfill a particular Bloodbather’s Appetite is determined by that Bloodbather’s dots in the Herd merit.
      Sanguine Will: May spend Vitae instead of Willpower to use Dread Powers, and may spend Willpower instead of other Vitae when using a Discipline. This does not grant a Vitae Pool, which must be acquired from another source for this to be applicable for the character.
      Potency: The character gains one dot of Potency as if he were a Horror, this affect Willpower Capacity, and Supernatural Tolerance. For those with a Major Template the Supernatural Tolerance does stack with that granted by the Major Template’s Power Stat.
      Sustained by Bloodbathing: Every month a Bloodbather goes without fulfilling their Appetite she loses access to a Willpower Dot, but upon fulfilling the Bloodbather’s Appetite she regains access to all the Willpower Dots she lost access to, in the state they were lost in(filled with Willpower Points or empty). Bloodbather also gain Willpower points by fulfilling her Appetite. Unlike Aspirations, Appetite does not grant Beats.
      Appetite: Perform their personally devised version of the bloodbath ritual.
      Bane: Being longer than a month since fulfilling Appetite.
      Dread Powers:
      Blood is Life:The Bloodbather isn’t bound by mortal laws of life and death. He’s not indestructible, per se, but if he’s killed by anything other than his Bane, he just comes back in a later scene like nothing happened. Even if he’s destroyed with his Bane, he’s never really gone — someone performing an Atrocity-based version blood bathing ritual in his name can always bring the him back for a sequel story.
      Blood is Eternal: This Dread Power literally raises the dead. The Dread Power costs 10 Willpower to use, and the activation roll is Intelligence + Occult. It only works on creatures with the Vampire Tag, and they come back with their Templates intact. The vampire raised from the dead appear from a dark deep shadow with the Confused, and Tainted Conditions, calculating the Diablerie victim as if it were themself.
      Allure of the Immortal: The Bloodbather forces a response from the pleasure centers of a living being’s brain, granting ecstatic visions, a feeling of communion with the universe, and sensations of bliss. The Dread Power costs 2 Willpower to activate. If successful, the victim suffers the Insensate Tilt (p. 285). If the victim fails a Resolve + Supernatural Tolerance roll, she gains a temporary derangement for the entity’s Power in days, in a form that binds her closer to the Bloodbather’s wishes.

      Storytelling Hints: Bloodbathers and the variety between different types put morality themes in stark contrast. Fundamental to these themes are questions like “How can the value of life be measured?” and “What would the consequences of removing the finality of death?”. While exploring themes that sprout up from

      Boogyman
      Stalking through the darkness, prowling outside of sight is the greatest source of fear, the unknown. It is the fact that nothing is scarier than letting the imagination fill in the unseen threatening presence that feeds and nourish Boogymen, the classic source of those bumps in the middle of the night.
      Becoming a Boogyman:
      A Boogyman requires terrifying a person to the point of being broken in madness to the point they are in a fugue through creeping Stealth but never revealing directly revealing his presence. The Boogyman-to-be (which uses the equipment building rules page CoD 100, Resolve + Stealth if the crafting is done under pressure in downtime) must spend at least seven nights terrorizing a person, repeatedly causing breaking points until the victim has all three of these Conditions: Broken, Fugue, and Madness. After the end of the psychological siege, the Boogyman-to-be spends a Willpower Dot to focus their mind on tasting the victim's fear and immediately loses a Dot of their Stability Advantage.
      The mechanical prerequisites to become a Boogyman vary in the following ways
      They standardly require a Social Merit representing the relationship to the person who is already a Boogyman which is instructing the character in how to become a Boogyman (even if the Boogyman is another Player Character or Support, though this is often done as a Mentor relationship, there is flexibility in which social merit is most appropriate), Stealth 3 and a Skill Specialization In Darkness. Someone who has no relationship to guide them may still stumble across the methods of becoming a Boogyman but require Stealth 4 and a Skill Specialization In Darkness.
      Tags Gained: Thought-Form, Vampire
      Sanguine Will: May spend Vitae instead of Willpower to use Dread Powers, and may spend Willpower instead of Vitae when called for in Disciplines. This does not grant a Vitae Pool, which must be acquired from another source for this to be applicable for the character.
      Potency: The character gains one dot of Potency as if he were a Horror, this affect Willpower Capacity, and Supernatural Tolerance. For those with a Major Template the Supernatural Tolerance does stack with that granted by the Major Template’s Power Stat.
      Feeds upon Fear: Every month a Boogyman goes without fulfilling their Appetite they lose access to a Willpower Dot, but upon fulfilling the Boogyman’s Appetite he regains access to all the Willpower Dots they lost access to, in the state they were lost in (filled with Willpower Points or empty). Boogymen also gain Willpower points by fulfilling their Appetite. Unlike Aspirations, Appetite does not grant Beats.
      Appetite: Inflict Spooked, Shaken, Madness, or Broken condition upon a stranger at night.
      Ban: Subject to Abjuration and Warding/Binding while using Creeping Shadow Dread Power, using Presence + Composure instead of Power + Resistance.
      Dread Powers:
      Creeping Shadow:The creature can turn itself into a shadow form by spending 1 Willpower becoming a shadow of itself.
      This form lasts for a scene or until the creature leaves it, allowing it to fly at its base Speed, slip through cracks, and flow around obstacles.
      Where you can't see: By spending 2 Willpower, the Boogyman can reach into the Gauntlet, the barrier between the material and spiritual world, and draw the barrier around himself as an instant action, concealing and hiding him just parallel to the physical world. The Boogyman is effectively undetectable; when he emerges by taking any action, he becomes detectable again. If he enters combat from his cloak by making an attack, then he adds a +3 bonus to its Dexterity + Stealth roll to make a surprise attack.
      Stare from the Dark:The Boogyman’s gaze induces madness and terror in its victims. By expending 1 Willpower and making a roll of an appropriate dice pool contested by the victim’s Composure, the Boogyman may inflict one of the following Conditions on the victim: Guilty, Shaken, or Spooked. For 3 Willpower he may instead inflict the Broken, Fugue, or Madness Conditions.

      Incubus/Succubus
      The story of the supremely sensual tempter/temptress draining away your vitality with a caress and drink your soul with a passionate kiss is ancient. Fear of those who can use their sexuality as a way to take everything important from you is more of a primordial terror than most modern people realize. This Minor Template is the embodiment of that fear, and uses sex appeal as a lethal weapon to feed upon others.
      Becoming a Incubus/Succubus:
      A Incubus/Succubus requires using sexual seduction to get someone to swear their Soul to you, sealing the promise with a kiss which also is used to suffocate them. The Incubus/Succubus-to-be (which uses the equipment building rules page CoD 100, Resolve + Persuasion if the crafting is done under pressure in downtime) must complete this deadly betrayal, which is always an Atrocity. After the murder, the Incubus/Succubus-to-be spends a Willpower Dot to focus their mind on drinking in the victim's Soul and immediately loses a Dot of their Stability Advantage.
      The mechanical prerequisites to become a Incubus/Succubus vary in the following ways
      They standardly require a Social Merit representing the relationship to the person who is already a Incubus/Succubus which is instructing the character in how to become a Incubus/Succubus (even if the Incubus/Succubus is another Player Character or Support, though this is often done as a Mentor relationship, there is flexibility in which social merit is most appropriate), Persuasion 3 and a Skill Specialization Seduction. Someone who has no relationship to guide them may still stumble across the methods of becoming a Incubus/Succubus but require Persuasion 4 and a Skill Specialization Seduction.
      Tags Gained: Vampire
      Sanguine Will: May spend Vitae instead of Willpower to use Dread Powers, and may spend Willpower instead of Vitae when called for in Disciplines. This does not grant a Vitae Pool, which must be acquired from another source for this to be applicable for the character.
      Potency: The character gains one dot of Potency as if he were a Horror, this affect Willpower Capacity, and Supernatural Tolerance. For those with a Major Template the Supernatural Tolerance does stack with that granted by the Major Template’s Power Stat.
      Soul Thirst: Every month a Incubus/Succubus goes without fulfilling their Appetite they lose access to a Willpower Dot, but upon fulfilling the Incubus/Succubus’s Appetite he regains access to all the Willpower Dots they lost access to, in the state they were lost in (filled with Willpower Points or empty). Incubus/Succubus also gain Willpower points by fulfilling their Appetite. Unlike Aspirations, Appetite does not grant Beats.
      Appetite: Use the Drink Soul Dread Power
      Bane: A person who is in "True Love", (Mechanically represented by mutual True Friend merits in a monogamous romantic partnership)
      Dread Powers:
      Seducer's Gaze: The Incubus/Succubus’s gaze can charm and beguile. When meeting the target’s gaze, she can spend 1 Willpower and roll Presence + Persuasion contested by the target’s Composure. If successful, the Incubus/Succubus counts as having a perfect impression against the target for Social maneuvers until the end of the scene. This power does not work on beings that qualify as a Bane for the Incubus/Succubus.
      Drink Soul: Incubus/Succubus are capable of stealing human souls for sustenance. To use this Dread Power the Incubus/Succubus must be in a passionate and consensual kiss with victim. Once that restriction is met, the Incubus/Succubus may spend 3 Willpower and roll an Manipulation + Persuasion dice pool, contested by the victim’s Resolve. If the Incubus/Succubus succeeds, it steals the victim’s soul and inflicts the Soulless Condition. When Stealing the Soul the Stability Advantage should be record for the Incubus/Succubus, for it Drink Vitality Dread Power. The victim (or her friends) may be able to find a way to restore her soul by killing the Incubus/Succubus, but loses any Stability Advantage of the Soul used to feed the Drain Vitality Dread Power. An Incubus/Succubus can hold as many souls as his Potency + Supernatural Tolerance.
      Drain Vitality: Through direct skin contact with a sensual caress Incubus/Succubus can steal Vitae or Willpower (chosen at activation) from ensoulled material beings. The activation roll of Manipulation + Persuasion + Specialization is contested by Stamina + Resolve + Supernatural Tolerance. Whichever character — entity or target — gains the most successes receives points of Willpower or Vitae equal to successes, while the other party loses the same number. Vitae may only go into existing Vitae Pool, but may be drained from any living being without a Vitae pool at the cost 1 Resistant Lethal per Vitae. This Dread Power also allow draining Stability Advantage from Souls the Incubus/Succubus has for Vitae or Willpower at the rate of 1 per Instant Action.

      Storyteller Note: Drain Vitality Dread Power allows Kindred vampires to continue to gain sustenance from mortals even after their normal vampiric feeding from humans becomes ineffective.

      Masquerader
      There is a surprising number of supernatural creatures that wish they could be a normal mortal, for a variety of reasons. These creatures
      Becoming a Masquerader:
      Opting into the role of being a Masquerader require crafting a mortal identity, complete with alternate appearance that becomes so finely detailed and real it takes on a life of its own in a preternatural sense. The Masquerader-to-be (which uses the equipment building rules page CoD 100, Resolve + Subterfuge if the crafting is done under pressure) must successfully masquerade as the Identity and perform an identity affirming act in a location Resonant with Truth which is Open. Upon completing that act the Masquerader-to-be spends a Willpower Dot and immediately loses a Dot of their Stability Advantage, which transforms their merely disguised identity into what is their Mortal Identity Dread Power.
      The mechanical prerequisites to become a Masquerader vary in the following ways
      If the person has the Vampire Tag they require Subterfuge 1 and a Skill Specialization False Lives. This is because becoming a Masquerader is innately intuitive for any of those that are already a vampire, and the little remaining is the mastery represent in that skill and specialty. If the person is not already a vampire, they require a Social Merit representing the relationship to the person who is already a Masquerader which is instructing the character in how to become a Masquerader (even if the Masquerader is another Player Character or Support, though this is often done as a Mentor relationship, there is flexibility in which social merit is most appropriate), Subterfuge 2 and a Skill Specialization False Lives. Someone who isn’t already a vampire and has no relationship to guide them may still stumble across the methods of becoming a Masquerader but require Subterfuge 4 and a Skill Specialization False Lives.
      Tags Gained: Shapeshifter
      Sanguine Will: May spend Vitae instead of Willpower to use Dread Powers, and may spend Willpower instead of Vitae when called for in Disciplines. This does not grant a Vitae Pool, which must be acquired from another source for this to be applicable for the character.
      Potency: The character gains one dot of Potency as if he were a Horror, this affect Willpower Capacity, and Supernatural Tolerance. For those with a Major Template the Supernatural Tolerance does stack with that granted by the Major Template’s Power Stat.
      Pull of the Mundane: Every month a Masquerader goes without fulfilling their Appetite they lose access to a Willpower Dot, but upon fulfilling the Masquerader’s Appetite he regains access to all the Willpower Dots they lost access to, in the state they were lost in (filled with Willpower Points or empty). Masqueraders also gain Willpower points by fulfilling their Appetite. Unlike Aspirations, Appetite does not grant Beats.
      Appetite: Living a week as the Mortal Identity without changing back to true form
      Dread Powers:
      Steal Identity: The Masquerader can steal the face, skin, and identity of Non-Template. She must kill the victim first, then spend 2 Willpower, and merge the features of the corpse with its own, turning the corpse into the corpse of the Identity being abandoned as if it had died as the victim of this power died. This is always an Atrocity, and additionally causes the automatic loss of 1 Stability Advantage. The identity is permanently replaces the one used by the Mortal Identity Dread Power. Sanctity of Merits applies to the Merit points invested in the Mortal Identity when losing an old Identity and gaining a new one, with Merits not accounted for belonging to the New Identity being lost if Masquerader does not have enough Mortal Identity Merit Points to cover them.
      Mortal Identity: This Dread Power disguises a Masquerader as a human, who need not match race, sex, or body type of the original identity. Using the Dread Power costs 1 Willpower, and the human identity seeming lasts the power is deactivated, by the Masquerader, by someone countering this dread power, or by the Masquerader's death. The human identity is near flawless, automatically winning Clash of Wills with beings less powerful than Archmages for powers trying to detect them as other than human or pierce the identity. While in this Identity the character does not have access to any supernatural powers/abilities, or any Merits applicable when they are in their true form. Merits for this Identity must be purchased separately and the Masquerade doesn't have access to them when in their true form. The Masquerader's Banes and Bans do not apply while in their Mortal Identity, but all damage taken in this form is Resistant Damage. Interactions with Supports while in the Mortal Identity provide none of their positive nor negative consequences as they normally would (Earning XP, releasing Temporary Atrocity, causing Hurt/Exposure, ect...). Atrocity is tracked as if the Mortal Identity was a separate Non-Template without the ability to remove Temporary Atrocity and if it ever would gain a Permanent Atrocity that Identity is destroyed, and a new one must be stolen via Steal Identity Dread Power to be able to use this Dread Power again. Hunted and Surveilled Conditions only apply while not within their Mortal Identity.
      Innocuous: This Dread Power does not require a roll to activate and has no cost. The Masquerader is very good at being overlooked. Perception rolls to notice the Masquerader are penalized by two dice when not in its Mortal Identity.


      Willpower
      These templates are solely Willpower-Based, and associated with the world of mankind, where few things can be either as cruel or as kind as the human race. It is governed by the ideas, both the open and closed mind types. Unlike other Categories of Acquired Skill-based Minor Templates, in this category may purchase anything other than what is normally available for powers or merits.

      Hacker
      Hacking has a level of urban myth associated, and Hackers can be considered the sorcerors and shamans of our day and age dealing with forces few regular people fully understand. The idea of being hacked invokes the same feelings someone centuries ago may have felt if they thought they were hexed. The our world is filled with that which isn't commonly understood but is commonly used, and those who peek behind the technological curtain find horrors, wonders, and even more curtains to peek behind.
      Becoming a Hacker:
      A Hacker as defined by this Minor Template is more than being a person who bypasses security measures. It requires breaking into parts of the internet which contain things sane minds were never meant to know and building a Mainframe which acts as their own personal server to connect to that portion of the internet (which uses the equipment building rules page CoD 100, Resolve + Computers if the crafting is done under pressure) and the Hacker-to-be must create and maintain this in a location with a Resonant Condition for Computers, and Data. Upon completing the Mainframe the Hacker-to-be spends a Willpower Dot and immediately loses a Dot of their Stability Advantage. Should the Mainframe be moved to an unsuitable location, be damaged or destroyed they cannot use their Dread Powers until they rebuild a fresh one in a suitable location.
      The mechanical prerequisites to become a Hacker vary in the following ways
      If the person is a non-template they require Computer 1 and a Skill Specialization Hacking; This is because becoming a Hacker is more innately intuitive for those without the distraction of other avenues of power, and the little remaining is the mastery represent in that skill and specialty. If the person already has a Major Template, they require a Social Merit representing the relationship to the person who is already a Hacker which is instructing the character in how to become a Hacker (even if the Hacker is another Player Character or Support, though this is often done as a Mentor relationship, there is flexibility in which social merit is most appropriate), Computers 2 and a Skill Specialization Hacking. Someone who already has a Major Template and has no relationship to guide them may still stumble across the methods of becoming a Hacker but require Computers 4 and a Skill Specialization Hacking.
      Tags Gained: Erudite
      Potency: The character gains one dot of Potency as if he were a Horror, this affect Willpower Capacity, and Supernatural Tolerance. For those with a Major Template the Supernatural Tolerance does stack with that granted by the Major Template’s Power Stat.
      Inquiring Mind: Every month a Hacker goes without fulfilling their Appetite they lose access to a Willpower Dot, but upon fulfilling the Hacker’s Appetite he regains access to all the Willpower Dots they lost access to, in the state they were lost in (filled with Willpower Points or empty). Hackers also gain Willpower points by fulfilling their Appetite. Unlike Aspirations, Appetite does not grant Beats.
      Appetite: Discover a secret through Hacking which is a breaking point.
      Dread Powers:
      Impossible Access: The Hacker can use any device with a display to which they connect to their Mainframe as a remote viewing screen. The Hacker is able to see through any digital camera the hacker specifically knows of as if it had a direct feed to the Hacker's Mainframe. Using this ability requires the expenditure of 1 Willpower per hour. The Hacker remains aware of its surroundings while surveilling.
      Technological Mastery: With a device with as little as a Smartphone in hardware abilities, electrical devices can be caused to fail in the Hacker’s presence. Lights flicker, cell phones get no reception, televisions and electronic machinery randomly turn on and off. In addition, the creature may spend 1 Willpower to either disable any device with an electrical components or seize control of such a device. This effect can turn an equipment bonus into a penalty or, if the device is capable of causing harm, lets the Hacker's attack a character holding or standing near the device using an appropriate dice pool. The attack deals lethal damage and uses the device’s equipment bonus as a damage modifier.
      Through a Scanner Darkly: By uploading a clear simple digital picture of the target to their Mainframe a Hacker can see into the depths of its victim’s soul. By spending 1 Willpower, it automatically learns the victim’s Virtue, Vice, Aspirations, and current Stability Advantage. By spending an additional Willpower point, it learns the circumstances of the character’s most recent failed breaking point. Subsequent Willpower points reveal older breaking points. If the Hacker uses its knowledge against the victim, it earns an exceptional success on a roll of three successes or more.


      Hunter
      Monsters roam the night, and that simple fact produces individuals that would stand against them. Being a monster is more than being a supernatural being, being a monster is carrying the weight of atrocity after atrocity until they irrevocably stain its very nature. It seems the cosmos hates monsters and Hunters can be considered their antibodies.
      Becoming a Hunter:
      A Hunter as defined by this Minor Template is to have a preternatural dedication for hunting atrocity committing monsters. It requires tracking down a supernatural monster who has Permanent Atrocity and confronting it in a way that removes its ability to perform more atrocities (which uses the equipment building rules page CoD 100, Resolve + Investigation if the crafting is done under pressure and during down time) and then the Hunter-to-be must swear an oath on their very soul to continue to do so. Upon completing the oath the Hunter-to-be spends a Willpower Dot and immediately loses a Dot of their Stability Advantage.
      The mechanical prerequisites to become a Hunter vary in the following ways
      If the person is a non-template they require Investigation 1 and a Skill Specialization Crime Scenes. This is because becoming a Hunter is more innately intuitive for those most likely to victims of supernatural power, and the little remaining is the mastery represent in that skill and specialty. If the person already has a Major Template, they require a Social Merit representing the relationship to the person who is already a Hunter which is instructing the character in how to become a Hunter (even if the Hunter is another Player Character or Support, though this is often done as a Mentor relationship, there is flexibility in which social merit is most appropriate, in particular Hunters tend to belong to Mystery Cult Initiations called Conspiracies), Investigation 2 and a Skill Specialization Crime Scenes. Someone who already has a Major Template and has no relationship to guide them may still stumble across the methods of becoming a Hunter but require Investigation 4 and a Skill Specialization Crime Scenes.
      Tags Gained: Faithful
      Potency: The character gains one dot of Potency as if he were a Horror, this affect Willpower Capacity, and Supernatural Tolerance. For those with a Major Template the Supernatural Tolerance does stack with that granted by the Major Template’s Power Stat.
      Can't lay down the Vigil: Every month a Hunter goes without fulfilling their Appetite they lose access to a Willpower Dot, but upon fulfilling the Hunter’s Appetite he regains access to all the Willpower Dots they lost access to, in the state they were lost in (filled with Willpower Points or empty). Hunters also gain Willpower points by fulfilling their Appetite. Unlike Aspirations, Appetite does not grant Beats.
      Appetite: Kill or Capture/Enslave a being with the Hunted Condition
      Dread Powers:
      Can't Hide from the Vigil: No matter how secure those with the Hunted Conditions think they are, by spending 1 Willpower the Hunter may suddenly appear in the scene in the same are they could use their Surveillance Dread Power on. This manifests in-game as unncanny planning, lucky preparation, clever investigation and possible preplacement just outside what is visible in the scene with proper equipment to enter. Any character witnessing this entrance must succeed on a reflexive Resolve + Composure roll or gain the Shaken Condition.
      If the characters have actively taken appropriate measures to keep the Hunter out (barring and locking the door, posting NPC guards, etc.) it will take the Hunter one full turn to break through, giving the characters time to react. If not, it’s just there as a reflexive action and can take one more action before anyone can react.
      Surveillance: The Hunter has a specific form of remote viewing he can practice on any being in the same city or general rural area that has the Surveilled Condition. The specifics varies by Hunter and might be able to see through any surveillance camera attached to a network he is accessing, or view from the eyes of a raven that he has put an implant in; perhaps he hears the whispers of Demons telling him what they observe from the Pit. This ability is always flavored by any Hunter Conspiracy the Hunter belongs to. Using this ability requires the expenditure of 1 Willpower per hour. The creature remains aware of its surroundings while surveilling.
      Know the Hunted: The Hunter can sense the presence of the Hunted Condition from a distance. The base range is two miles per Rank; Hunters may spend an Willpower to multiply this by 10. If successful on a Wits roll, the Hunter becomes aware of the direction and distance to the nearest being with the Hunted Condition in range.

      Ideologue
      The world is filled with competing ideologies, none of which are based in reality because ideology by its nature of being an abstract reductionist models because they a mental constructs of finite minds in a complex infinite universe. Any ideology that could be fathomed by a finite mind is by nature inaccurate, but this does not stop from becoming massive movements, or from individuals from pinning their defining identity and unwavering faith in them. The people willing to transcend sanity to become Ideologues in pursuit of their ideology suffer greatly when confronted with irrefutable proof of its flaws, and those that carry the weight of Permanent Atrocity are actually damaged by such proof. This causes an Ideologue to eschew any reasonable examination of or confrontation against their views with a rabid ferocity that demands Atrocity against those that disagree with them.
      Becoming a Ideologue:
      An Ideologue requires zealotry, blind and deaf faith, and a terrifying desire to see unrealistic beliefs made reality no matter the cost. The Ideologue-to-be (which uses the equipment building rules page CoD 100, Resolve + Poltics if the crafting is done under pressure) must become active in an existing political movement (which may or may not also be religious). They must push in ever increasing fervor towards the goals of that movement until they suffer a Breaking Point directly caused by their actions supporting the movement and gain a Broken, Madness, or Fugue Condition as a result, the Ideologue-to-be spends a Willpower Dot to remain dedicated to the Cause and immediately loses a Dot of their Stability Advantage as the Cause consumes a little bit of their soul.
      The mechanical prerequisites to become a Ideologue vary in the following ways
      They standardly require a Social Merit representing the relationship to the person who is already a Ideologue which is instructing the character in how to become a Ideologue (even if the Ideologue is another Player Character or Support, though this is often done as a Mentor relationship, there is flexibility in which social merit is most appropriate), Poltics 3 and a Skill Specialization Having Fervor. Someone who has no relationship to guide them may still stumble across the methods of becoming a Ideologue but require Politics 4 and a Skill Specialization Having Fervor.
      Tags Gained: Faithful
      Potency: The character gains one dot of Potency as if he were a Horror, this affect Willpower Capacity, and Supernatural Tolerance. For those with a Major Template the Supernatural Tolerance does stack with that granted by the Major Template’s Power Stat.
      With us or against us!: Every month a Ideologue goes without fulfilling their Appetite they lose access to a Willpower Dot, but upon fulfilling the Ideologue’s Appetite he regains access to all the Willpower Dots they lost access to, in the state they were lost in (filled with Willpower Points or empty). Ideologue also gain Willpower points by fulfilling their Appetite. Unlike Aspirations, Appetite does not grant Beats.
      Appetite: Commit an Atrocity against someone who opposes your Ideology
      Bane: Being directly confronted with irrefutable proof of flaws with Ideology
      Dread Powers:
      Aggressive Ideology: The Ideologue speaks to a person with a Politics + Composure (they must be in a Condition capable of doing so), and plants an idea about the ideology that transformed the character into an Ideologue in their mind. When that person tells someone else the idea, it takes hold in their mind, too, as well as in the minds of whomever they tell. The Dread Power costs 7 Willpower to activate, and is contested by Resolve + Composure + Supernatural Tolerance against the original activation roll's successes.
      Hypnotic Rhetoric: The Ideologue can charm and beguile with their rhetoric. When speaking about either their ideology or the target’s, he can spend 1 Willpower and roll Presence + Politics contested by the target’s Composure. If successful, the Ideologue counts as having a perfect impression against the target for Social maneuvers until the end of the scene.
      Caviling Eyes: The Ideologue can sense if others they are speaking with measure up to their ideology. By spending 1 Willpower, he automatically learns the victim’s Virtue, Vice, Aspirations, and current Stability Advantage. By spending an additional Willpower point, he learns the circumstances of the character’s most recent failed breaking point which related to the Ideologue's ideology. Subsequent Willpower points reveal older breaking points of that nature. If the Ideologue uses his knowledge against the victim, it earns an exceptional success on a roll of three successes or more.


      Second Gunman
      There is something extremely emotional jarring about the type of death a sniper can inflict. Add the idea that there may be a secret conspiracy that conceals the culprit, while someone else takes the blame, and you have paranoia fuel enough to drive some people insane. Whatever the truth is, it seems like there is something in the myth of the Second Gunman even if it is never able to be definitively proven. The truth is out there.
      Second Gunman are real even though it'd be impossible to prove that to anyone who was even slightLY skeptical. The proof is all implied and none of it concrete. They are the silent red right hands of mysterious cults and secret societies and need to be such. A Second Gunman suffers without a Mystery Cult to command him to kill, and if he has gained Permanent Atrocity it can even kill him to be without it. They are enslaved to sacrificing their morality to the idea of the greater good presented by their Mystery Cult Initiation. Due to their clandestine byzantine nature Mystery Cults usually use their influence to ensure there is an obvious gunman, usually someone mentally ill and provoked with mystical or psychological means, to take the blame for the death the Second Gunman ensures is a successful excecution and not merely an assassination attempt.
      Becoming a Second Gunman:
      An Second Gunman requires loyalty to an Mystery Cult willing to kill to achieve their goal, and a professional level of shooting with particular excellence in sniping. The Second Gunman-to-be (which uses the equipment building rules page CoD 100, Resolve + Fire Arms if the crafting is done under pressure in downtime) must snipe and kill an Ensouled being on the orders of superiors in a Mystery Cult Initiation. After the kill the Second Gunman-to-be spends a Willpower Dot to dedicate themselves to being the hidden hand of death for those that command them and immediately loses a Dot of their Stability Advantage as their devotion to the Mystery Cult consumes a little bit of their soul.
      The mechanical prerequisites to become a Second Gunman vary in the following ways
      They standardly require a Social Merit representing the relationship to the person who is already a Second Gunman which is instructing the character in how to become a Second Gunman (even if the Second Gunman is another Player Character or Support, though this is often done as a Mentor relationship, there is flexibility in which social merit is most appropriate), Fire Arms 3 and a Skill Specialization Sniper. Someone who has no relationship to guide them may still stumble across the methods of becoming a Second Gunman but require Fire Arms 4 and a Skill Specialization Sniper.
      Tags Gained: Erudite
      Potency: The character gains one dot of Potency as if he were a Horror, this affect Willpower Capacity, and Supernatural Tolerance. For those with a Major Template the Supernatural Tolerance does stack with that granted by the Major Template’s Power Stat.
      Always waiting for next mission: Every month a Second Gunman goes without fulfilling their Appetite they lose access to a Willpower Dot, but upon fulfilling the Second Gunman’s Appetite he regains access to all the Willpower Dots they lost access to, in the state they were lost in (filled with Willpower Points or empty). Second Gunman also gain Willpower points by fulfilling their Appetite. Unlike Aspirations, Appetite does not grant Beats.
      Appetite: Fatally snipe an Ensouled being on orders of a superior with the authority to make such orders with which you maintain Mystery Cult Initiation.
      Bane: Having no Mystery Cult Initiation with an Mystery Cult that will authorize you to kill.
      Dread Powers:
      A Face in the Crowd: This Dread Power does not require a roll to activate and has no cost. The Second Gunman is very good at being overlooked. Perception rolls to notice the Second Gunman are penalized by two dice, this explicitly stacks with What Proof? Dread powers.
      Grassy Knoll: By spending 2 Willpower, the Second Gunman can reach into the Gauntlet, the barrier between the material and spiritual world, and draw the barrier around himself as an instant action, concealing and hiding him just parallel to the physical world. The Second Gunman is effectively undetectable; when he emerges by taking any action, he becomes detectable again. If he enters combat from his cloak by making an attack, then he adds a +3 bonus to its Dexterity + Stealth roll to make a surprise attack.
      What Proof?: The Second Gunman leaves little trace in his surrounding environment, making investigating him that much more difficult; this applies a -3 penalty on all rolls to perceive him indirectly or to gather Clues about him in scenes where he has been, increased to -6 if he spends an instant action in that scene to further clean up after himself.


      Minor Templates
      Where Major Templates are things often bestowed upon a character by forces and/or beings beyond a person’s control Minor Templates are the sources of supernatural power that can be mastered with dedication, effort, and the correct secrets. Several Minor Templates are even available to a formerly non-template character. Going through with the rituals to unlock the becoming a Minor Template fundamentally alters a person’s soul to perform

      Originally posted by New Condition
      Sleepwalker (Persistent)
      You are fitful in your slumber, you know and accept on some level that everyone has magical potential and that knowledge and acceptance keeps the weight of the Abyss from your soul. You do not count as a Sleeper witness for Supernal Beings or Magick. Sentient beings without the Mage Template or the Supernal Tag count as Sleepers unless they have this condition.
      Cause: Being the target of a Prime 3 Stealing Fire effect, Starting to understand a Supernal Mystery, Undergoing a spiritual journey/pilgrimage
      Resolution: Awakening, or choosing to disbelieve that everyone has magic potential, and thus falling back Asleep
      Beat: When being exposed to Supernal Magick causes tragedy and hardship for you and your loved ones.
      Originally posted by New Condition
      Obliterated (Persistent)
      More than dead, this being has been sacredly murdered that that their continued existence is anathema to all realities anywhere. The being with this Condition gains a Bane which is triggered simply by existing and counts their Permanent Atrocity as if it were one higher than it actually is for purposes of that Bane. This Condition also applies to any ephemeral or physical beings which are copies of that being such as Ghosts, crafted Spirits or Geotia and even clones with the memories of the original. The Bane leads to Resistant Aggravated damage for simply existing. This condition lasts past death, making any Resurrection of a being with this Condition very brief and painful for the resurrected being.
      Cause: Being the subject of a Sacred Murder Dread Power, Being the target of certain Cosmic forces of destruction and judgment
      Resolution: None, the Karmic debt needed to qualify to be the target of this condition is never paid in full.
      Originally posted by New Condition
      Ghouled (Persistent)
      Your character has accepted blood/Vitae from a vampire, and this supernaturally obligates you into doing what the vampire wishes. Note this Condition as Ghouled (Character). You may have Ghouled multiple times, for different characters.
      Once per chapter, that character may request something of yours, and you may gain a beat if your character does as requested, without rolling to resist. Attacks pools or Hard Leverage Social Maneuvers against a character to whom you have the Ghouled Condition are always a Chance Die.
      Cause: Fulfilling the Ghoul Ritual Bloodbather Appetite, A vampire with less total Supernatural Tolerance intentionally feeding from another vampire with more total Supernatural Tolerance
      Resolution: Undergoing a certain mystical treatments.
