          Compunction, Hesitation, and Post Traumatic Stress Disorder
          To initiate using lethal force during a scene that would be a breaking point for your characters Stability Advantage if the subjects of your violence were to die you must succeed a reflexive Resolve + Composure check, or spend a point of Willpower. If you fail you may spend an Instant Action to reroll (with the standard -1 for the previous failure). All further re-rolls within the same scene to avoid natural compunction against this type of violence are also instant actions. This does not apply when they character is acting on compulsions caused by the supernatural powers of others, or internal supernatural urges beyond their control. Being supernaturally controlled towards this type of violence, whether internal or externally, is always a breaking point due to its traumatic nature and damage to sense of identity.

          Should a character need to initiate lethal force when they are out of Willpower, they may do so but must take one of the following Conditions at the end of the Scene: Guilty, Shaken, or Spooked. If they already have all these conditions they instead choose from this list: Broken, Fugue, or Madness. If they are unfortunate enough to have all those conditions they instead roll three basic stress against a Support. Once a character has only has one Support left they take a Minor Undertaking as a Slasher, and when a character has no Supports they take Major Undertaking as a Slasher and no longer count as an ensoulled being with breaking points. For more details check the Slasher Section.

          This mechanic reflects that character's within this iteration of World of Darkness are not video game characters that perpetrate gruesome violence easily without care, and to also increase the value of refreshing Willpower for violent characters, which fits trope of within retelling of the stories characters that regularly engage in battle which is portrayed as gritty, to include real life war-stories, and some police dramas. (think of people indulging vices to "blow off steam" or commit acts of charity because "I had to make sense of it all") It also increases how dangerous creatures that lack breaking points that allow violence seem compared to those that have them; it literally costs them nothing to harm others. It also highlights how compulsions from supernatural forces towards violence are even more frightening for the loss of control. This also helps portray the mental stresses of combat, and battle fatigue.


          Supports

          This system is a variant of the standard Integrity/Harmony/Humanity/Wisdom/Clarity/Synergy system that tracks a being's ability to cope and blend in with mundane society by a number of personally meaningful relationships with mundane people that character can maintain. It produces a rich cast of NPCs which matter to the characters, both mechanically and for story reasons. Each character begins game with seven such relationships. These relationships need not be positive, and in fact may be downright hostile but if the relationship ended the character would feel a deep personal loss. These relationships provide advantages, temptations, conflicts, Sane Experiences, and vulnerable points that others can try to exploit to each playable character. Each Support is a Minor or Non-Template character with whom a character has a relationship that helps maintain their sanity. Multiple characters may claim the same Minor or Non-Template character as a Support, with the same or different types of relationships and this is a great way to make character ties.

          Players should be encouraged to diversify their Supports to flesh out their character to be more than two dimensional, because relationships complicate people.

          When calculating a character's Integrity/Harmony/Humanity/Wisdom/Clarity/Synergy, instead use the remaining Full or Empty Supports the character has. Please see Support Loss to explain these concepts.

          Touchstones and Supports:
          Instead of choosing anything as Touchstones characters instead choose one support as their Touchstone. For Uratha they choose one Human and one Spirit Touchstone.

          Support Loss:
          Supports can be lost in a variety of ways, but the types of loss can be categorized in two ways. Loss that allows the character to go through the process of replacing the Support without cost and loss that requires Sane Experiences to recover from before they can replace the Support. Think of a Support as a jar of fluid, the fluid is the specific person (or in the case of Uratha, possibly a gaffling spirit) and the jar represents the ability to hold that relationship. Some losses simply empty the jar and it can be refilled overtime with tears, but other types of loss shatter the jar and requires going out to find a replacement. Supports that are loss through Hurt alway require Sane Experience to recover. Supports lost through the wiles of fate, or after they have been fully Exposed may be replaced without cause if the character goes through Grief. As a second option the character may choose the path of Vengance when they lose a Support to the wiles of fate or after they are Exposed, in which case they also require Sane Experience to replace.

          Hurt and Exposed Support
          Stress and Exposure are system abstractions for tracking two different dangers to Supports. Both Hurt is caused by Stress and Exposed is caused by Exposure in the same way Wounds are caused by Damage. Hurt is damage to the relationships itself and its meaning to the character. Like Wounds there are three levels of severity: Basic, Lethal, and Aggravated. """Copy Paste Bashing/Lethal/Aggravated marking system Here"""

          Each Support begins with three boxes to track Hurt and three boxes to track Exposed, possibly modified by their Kink and Comfort.

          Stress is the roll that can result in Hurt to the Support. Hurt is damage to the relationship and the character's ability to connect with the Support. Stress has several sources.

          First is from certain types Kinks cause Stress to the Support by making the Support start with Aggravated Hurt.

          Roll Aggravated Hurt for each Support at the beginning of each chapter. If it is a successful take one of the Conditions listed for the kink that lasts until resolved (Count as Sanity Beats for resolving these conditions). If the kink doesn't list conditions instead treat as a Basic Stress Roll against that Support.

          When a character fails a Breaking Point they gain a Condition (See Breaking Points for more information) and Roll 3 Basic Stress against a Support. This reflects internal struggles within the character and may have an outward manifestation in a small narrated conflict between the Support and character.

          When a character dramatically fails a Breaking Point they gain a Condition (See Breaking Points for more information) and Roll 5 Lethal Stress against a Support.

          Once per game a player may call for a small narrated scene and roll the character's current total Temporary Atrocity as Basic Stress against a Support (if the scene fits the Supports Kink subtract 2 dice from the die pool). If the roll is a success or exceptional success, all current Temporary Atrocity are removed from the character, however on an exceptional success the roll is treated as Lethal Stress instead. If successful the scene always includes horrific hints to the character's monstrous nature that the Support ignores if the stress is insuffient to cause the Support to be lost.

          Roll Aggravated Exposed for each Support at the beginning of each chapter. Treat as a Basic Exposure Roll against that Support.

          Healing Hurt and Exposed Supports
          Healing Hurt to Support may be done once a chapter if the player opts to narrate a brief scene that reflects the strengthening of the relationship, spend a Willpower point or take an appropriate negative Condition with storyteller permission to make a social skill roll that is applicable to their relationship. Each success heals one lethal stress or two basic stress. Healing Aggravated Hurt is best left to specific story arch rewards. The beat gained from overcoming a condition gain in this way is a Sanity beat.

          Vengeance
          If a Support dies, either through the wiles of fate or because they are fully exposed, the character has an option to choose to seek Vengeance or to suffer Grief. Seeking Vengeance is very empowering, but requires a stated target for Atrocity and essentially burns out the place in the character's heart where they held their feelings for their Support. The Support for which a character chooses the path of Vengeance must be purchased with Sane Experience to be replaced. Support loss through Hurt, even if that Hurt is reflected in the death of of the Support in a fit of rage, do not provoke Vengeance and may not be recovered through Grief even though it provokes the same penalties of grief.

          The character trades out a Personal Aspiration towards their stated goal of Vengeance of committing an Atrocity towards a defined target which they can blame for the loss of the Support, and gains the Obsession Condition towards that aspiration. As long as the character maintains the Personal Aspiration to punish the focus of their Vengeance with Atrocities they can not resolve this Obsession condition.

          Grief and filling Empty Supports
          When a character loses a Support they may suffer Grief, or (if possible) pursue Vengeance. Grief is represented by the Broken Condition. Grief caused by losing a Support through Hurt does not bare fruit in the form of replacing the Support without Sane Experience cost, but the character can spend Sane Experience to replace the Support, which is one way to resolve the Broken Condition. Going through grief when the Support is lost in other ways allows emotional healing and lets the character replace the Support without Sane Experience cost when they resolve the Broken condition gained in this way either by gaining a new support with Sane Experience Purchase or an Exceptional Success on a Break Roll. This represents the flourishing of personal relationships after healthily dealing with tragedy. This may be done by choosing the same Support again if the Support is alive and able to fill that role, or choosing a new relationship with a new individual.

          The character may opt out of the Broken Condition gained in this way, which forfeits gaining the Support back for free for supports lost through being Exposed or through the wiles of fate, or inflict 3 basic stress distributed among their remaining Supports as the character deems fit for Supports lost through Hurt.


          Kinks
          Meaningful relationships are complicated, and in the World of Darkness this is especially true. Relationships with Supports always have a Kink, an aspect with brings conflict and is an expression of what is or is potentially dysfunctional about the relationship. As Aggravated Hurt increases the Kink becomes more and more pronounced in the relationship. Without Aggravated Hurt the Kink is a subtle undercurrent, a relationship blind spot both the character and Support might not consciously know is wrong, but by the time the last Hurt Box is filled with Aggravated hurt the Kink is what shatters the relationship. As indicated above roll Aggravated Hurt for each Support at the begining of each chapter. If the roll is a successful take one of the Conditions listed for the kink that last until resolved (Count as Sanity Beats for resolving these conditions). If the kink doesn't list conditions instead treat as a Basic Stress Roll against that Support. When a kink expressed as a choice between two Conditions, usually the first condition implies that the character is acting upon the Support, and the second that they are acted upon by the Support. Beats gained by overcoming Conditions gained from Support Kinks count as Sanity beats.

          Abuse: The relationship is marred by inappropriate violent emotional and physical outbursts. Choose between having the Guilty or Embarressing Secret Condition. Guilty means your character is the abuser in this instance and suffers remorse over harming the Support, and Embarressing Secret means they allow the Support to abuse them.
          Betrayal: The relationship has repeated severe violations of trust. Choose between having the Guilty or Notoriety Condition. Guilty means the character is the one violating a meaningful tenent of trust in their relationship and Notoriety means their Support has publicly done that shames the character or revealed personal and private unpleasant truths about the character. to those outside the relationship.
          Codependent: The relationship with the Support is defined by serious emotional interdependence. Unlike other kinks the difference between the first Condition and second Condition is whether or not the character is getting her 'enough' time with their Support. Choose between having the Swooning or Deprived Condition focused on the Support.
          Controlling: The relationship is one with a struggle for power over the other. This can be a stalker's obsession, unchecked jealousy, or zealous over-protectiveness. Choose between having the Obsession or Leveraged. Obsession means the character is extremely focused on controlling Support. Leveraged is that the character has loss control to the Support, which uses the Leveaged Condition to isolate the character from outside interaction.
          Distance: The relationship has emotional or physical distance which acts a wall between the character and Support. This might be because of dissimilarity, callousness on the part of the character/Support, or having to communicate long distance and only infrequent visits.Support has 4 Exposed Boxes instead of 3, Starts with additional Aggravated Hurt
          Forbidden: Relationships which others would deny us are more intense. This intensity fuels the emotions which underlie the relationship. While the relationship can still implode from Stress, it has a longer fuse. The Support has 4 Hurt Boxes instead of 3, Starts with additional Aggravated Exposed
          Horror: Guilty or Spooked Condition
          Insanity:
          Rejection: Support has 2 Hurt Boxes instead of 3. May not choose Acceptance Comfort.

          Comfort
          Each relationship provided by a support also provide some type of advantage. This has the dual purpose of making the player value the relationship, and to flesh out the character's place in the world as defined by their relationships. The merits provided by a Support do not count toward Cover-Up for both balance reasons and because the character is intimately tied with their personal life with the Support by the nature of the relationship, and do not stack with each other free sources of merits but do stack with merits purchased at character creation or with experience.

          Acceptance: Your Support is exceptionally understanding and nonjudgemental of you and your faults. This in return has deepened your feelings for them. This Support has an additional Hurt box.
          Access: The Support provides access to an otherwise restricted place, equivilant to a two-dot Safe-place. The Support does this without leaving a papertrail back to the character.
          Assistance: The Support will physically come to the character’s aid, serving as a one-dot Retainer.
          Bankroll: The Support has funds, access to funds, or goods/equipment which she can supply to the character, granting what amounts to one dot of Resources.
          Discreet: Your Support is knows how to keep secrets and understands that you want your relationship to remain private. This helps keep them safe against those that would use them against you. This Support has additional Exposed box.
          Info: The Support has access to useful information, and can provide the equivalent of one dot of Contacts.
          Nepotism: The Support helps you hold a position in an organization, equivalent to a one-dot Status.
          Sounding-board: After spending time speaking with Support about a particular problem gain the Inspired Condition concerning that problem.

          Support Rules to interface with Alternate Harmony Rules

          Breaking Points toward the Spirit:
          Dramatic Failure: The character's worldview has been damaged, perhaps beyond repair. The character suffers from traumatic stress. Roll 5 Hurt against a Support and choose from the following Conditions (or create a new one with Storyteller approval): Broken, Fugue, or Madness. Also, take a Beat.

          Failure: The character's worldview has been shaken and he probably questions his sense of self, his ability to relate to people, his own moral worth, or his sanity. Roll 3 Hurt against a Support and choose one of the following Conditions (or create a new one with Storyteller approval): Guilty, Shaken, or Spooked.

          Success: The character has come through the breaking point intact. He might feel guilty or upset about what happened, but he can cope. Choose one of the following Conditions (or create a new one with Storyteller approval): Guilty, Shaken, or Spooked.

          Exceptional Success: The character somehow manages not only to survive the breaking point, but to find meaning in it, to reaffirm his own self-worth, or to pass through fire and become tempered by it. The character takes a Sane Beat and regains a point of Willpower.

          Breaking Points toward the Flesh
          Dramatic Failure: The character's worldview has been damaged, perhaps beyond repair. The character suffers from traumatic stress. Gain an Empty Support and choose from the following Conditions (or create a new one with Storyteller approval): Broken, Fugue, or Madness. Also, take a Beat.

          Failure: The character's worldview has been shaken and he probably questions his sense of self, his ability to relate to people, his own moral worth, or his sanity. Gain an Empty Support and choose one of the following Conditions (or create a new one with Storyteller approval): Guilty, Shaken, or Spooked.

          Success: The character has come through the breaking point intact. He might feel guilty or upset about what happened, but he can cope. Choose one of the following Conditions (or create a new one with Storyteller approval): Guilty, Shaken, or Spooked.

          Exceptional Success: The character somehow manages not only to survive the breaking point, but to find meaning in it, to reaffirm his own self-worth, or to pass through fire and become tempered by it. The character takes a Sane Beat and regains a point of Willpower.

          Permanent Hurt
          This lists sources of Aggravated Hurt that automatically apply to a support when they become applicable:
          • Support has knowledge the Character is a Supernatural being 1
          • Support has a Minor Template 1


          Permanent Exposure
          This lists sources of Aggravated Exposure that automatically apply to a support when they become applicable:
          • Support has a Minor Template 2

          Exposure
          Exposure of Supports may happen in one of two ways, either the character does something to reveal or risk revealing the importance of the relationship to the supernatural community or a member of the supernatural community deliberately investigates into the mundane people that the character cares about. This can be done through social maneuvering and gaining clues against a character.
          Each door opened or clue gained against a character may be turned in for 1 basic Expose against a Support of that character, chosen by the player of the character whose Support it is. If the character investigating or socially plying the character fills the last Expose box of a Support they may narrate a scene with the storyteller what they do with the support they have discovered after the current scene is over which reflects the defensive character's loss of the Support. This may mean they apply the Conspirator condition to the support if they themselves are able to purchase it with Sane Experience as a Support, has an empty Support for which they are finished grieving, purchase with Story Experience as a merit, or kill the Support and possibly provoke vengeance.

          ----

          Alternate Harmony where "High Harmony" stat means having more Harmony between spirit and flesh
          Uratha are drawn back and forth between their role as apex spirit predator maintaining the balance of the spirit ecology and as beings of flesh in need of human connections. For an Uratha being pulled away from the spirit world is as dangerous to their emotional and mental well being as slipping away from the mundane normal world. To reflect the complex pull towards two different aspects of their nature, which is how Harmony is expressed, Uratha have two types of Supports. These Supports are capped at 5 each, for a total of 10. The first type is Human Supports which are the same as other supernaturals and mortals. The other type of Support is Spirit Supports which represents minor gaffling spirits within their territory which are evident of the spiritual health of aspects their territory. Think of these spirits as ecological indicator species found in the natural world. Like indicator species in the natural world the presence of these low level and often bottom of the food chain spirits show what the environmental factors foster. If these spirits are overfed and progress beyond gaffling shows imbalance, or if they are lost through through hurt, exposure, or vengeance they also indicate damage to the ecology. In these cases the damage represents neglect or abuse by the Uratha (Hurt), over exploitation or deliberate outside sabotage (Exposure) or reckless damage caused by reactionary rampage (Vengeance). Watching over, interacting with and keeping these spirits in line is the spiritual equivalent for a Uratha as maintaining their relationship with their territory. Uratha instinctively feel maintaining the status qua or equilibrium with these spirits keeps their territories, and thus their lives in balance, and they are correct that is a major factor in doing so.

          Uratha begin play with 4 Human Supports and 3 Spirit Supports. After their first change some human relationships fall away, and a new spiritual awareness awakens.


          Human Relationships
          Parent
          Sibling
          Child
          Lover
          Rival
          Mentor
          Protege

          Spirit Relationships
          Virtue
          Vice
          Artifical
          Nature
          Elemental
          Magath



          Spirit Supports
          At 4 or more Spirit Supports they don't need to roll to enter the Hisil.
          -1 to Rolls to enter the Material each.
          At 1 or less Spirit Supports takes the Primal Urge in Lethal wounds when changing forms.
          At 0 Spirit Supports the Uratha loses the ability to reach to the Hisil

          Human Support
          At 4 or more Human Supports they don't need to roll to enter the Material
          -1 to Rolls to enter the Hisil each.
          At 1 or less Human Supports treats all Banes as if had 1 Permanent Atrocity higher.
          At 0 Human Supports the Uratha loses the ability to reach to the Material


          Uratha are not like police. Police merely react to infractions against the laws without concern. Uratha are are much closer to being both game-wardens and the hunters, they react to infractions that endanger but also set population limits that fosters the ecologic health and then thin populations.

              Applicable Experience
              Experience Points are spend toward what they are Purchasing when they are earn, this is to promote organic character growth based on what is happening during the story and freshly within the player's mind. This is recorded on a dated index card representing each experience in a box dedicated to each character.

              Earned experience is spent immediately and is recorded on the index card, but not recorded as a number of the character sheet in the 'spent' column until the purchase is finalized, ie experience that only partially pay for something isn't recorded on the character sheet until the final needed is spent. This is to help keep track of partial expenditures so they don't fall between the cracks. Also Experience are segregated into different types to incentivize different character activities to grow in different ways. How the character earns these experience is both fitting and helps shapes the setting towards canon.

              Story Experience is earned from Story Beats gotten for Personal Aspirations, most normal beats, and may be spent on Merits and Specializations.

              Sane Experience is earned from Sanity Beats gotten from Conditions gained from healing Hurt to Support, other from Conditions cause by Support Kinks. It may be spent on replacing Permanent Willpower and Supports.

              Fundamental Experience earned from beats earned from Attendance, each chapter attended is a Fundamental beat. Fundamental Experience is spent on Skills and Attributes, the aspects of a character that most define who they are and what they are capable of and as such should be the most stable part of the character. Limiting access to improving these parts of the character makes character creation a more important part of defining who the character fundamentally is, and increasing these aspects increase the base capacity of the character, and are thus a good gauge of general competency. Also limiting growth in these internal aspects of the character which do not directly interface with the setting promotes experience growth in stats that directly reflect the character's connections to the setting. Character's who are "good at everything" are not as interesting as characters with defined limitations and strengths and this makes better characters in the end.

              Splat Experience is earned primarily through pursuing Shared Aspirations added to the character by the template. It has several types and is exlained in the Shared Aspirations section.

              Shared Aspirations
              Each splat gain three Aspirations beyond their three Personal Aspirations, for mages these are Arcane Aspirations which is a source of Arcane Beats and thus Arcane Experience, vampires have Requiem Aspirations which is a source of Requiem Beat and thus Requiem Experience, werewolves have Primal Aspirations which are a source of Primal Beats and thus Primal Experience, changelings have Stray Aspirations which are a source of Stray Beats and thus Stray Experience, Sin-Eaters have Ossurary Aspirations which are a source of Ossurary Beats and thus Ossurary Experience. These different aspirations often define the unifying goals of the groups they represent, and/or promote styles of play conducive to maintaining themes and motifs presented in Chronicles of Darkness canon. These Aspirations are by definition implied to already exist in the source material but in Frailty Chronicles we assign mechanical weight to them to reward playing to type and promote play consistent with the canon setting. Also let it be pointed out these are the types of Experiences that are spent on supernatural powers and benefits, which are often the most sought after mechanics to add to characters, so the motivation to play towards canon should be significant. Aspirations that naturally fit character types it promotes play consistent with those character types, and rewards playing towards type, making a more consistent setting. Many role players may want to play a special snowflake, but universal application of that trend is detrimental for a cohesive fiction or internally consistent world


              Arcane Experience is earned frame Arcane beats, which come from
              Arcane Aspirations
              These are Aspirations that automatically apply to a character of these paths and orders/ministries and are a source of Arcane beats
              Arcanthus: Uncover secrets about the nature of fate and time
              Mastigos: Uncover secrets about the nature of the mind and space
              Moros: Uncover secrets about the nature of death and the material world
              Obrimos: Uncover secrets about the nature of magic and cosmic energy
              Thyrsus: Uncover secrets about the nature of spirits and the natural world

              Adamantine Arrow: Refine and perfect the self and world through enlightened conflict.
              Guardians of the Veil: Maintain the conspiracy protecting the supernatural from the wilting touch of the mundane
              Myserium: Study and research profound mysteries and hidden knowledge
              Silver Ladder: Guide the Awakened community towards a new Atlantis through enlightened leadership.
              Free Council: Advance humanity towards freedom from the Lie through technology and politics.

              Hegemony: Maintain the Prison of the Lie through impersonal bureaucracy and enforcing conformity.
              Panopticon: Maintain the prison of the Lie through violating privacy, and spreading distrust.
              Paetrean: Maintain the prison of the Lie through military force and fear of violence.
              Patra: Maintain the prison of the Lie by enforcing dogmatic belief and spiritually dead piety

              Cabal/Pylon Aspiration: This Aspiration is chosen by Cabal Consensus, and is gained by the whole pack when earned. This is to reflect the enlightening affect when working towards mutual support to some higher purpose of a mystically themed group. (Cabals and Pylons should have occult symbolism and themes in how they work to reap this reward) This also encourages flavor styles of play.

              Primal Experience is earned from beats gotten from Conditions gain in the Sacred Hunt, and from pursuing Auspice, Tribe and Pack Aspirations

              Auspice/Pure Aspiration (Static)
              Irraka: Out-smart and out-maneuver the prey
              Ithaeur: Seek the deeper mysteries about the prey
              Elidoth: Respect the dignity of the prey
              Cahalith: Cause the prey to flee by letting it know that it is hunted
              Rahu: Kill the prey with swift mercy

              Pure: Punish the Forsaken for the death of Urfidum

              Tribe Aspirations (Static)
              Iron Masters: Respect your territory in all things
              Boneshadow: Repay a spirit for how it has treated you and yours
              Stormlord: Conceal your weakness from friend and foe
              Blood Talons: Bravely stand against foes
              Hunters in the darkness: Defend or avenge a sacred place

              Predator King: Achieve all goals without human tools
              Fire Touched: Always challenge falsehood
              Ivory Claws: Treat those of lesser lineage as lesser

              Pack Aspiration: This Aspiration is chosen by Pack Consensus, and is gained by the whole pack when earned. This is to promote teamwork and pack mentality.

              Sanguine Experience is earned from Sanguine Beats from pursuing from Clan, Covenant and Touchstone Aspirations

              Clan Aspiration: (Static)
              Daeva: Gather the love and adoration of other
              Gangrel: Withstand all trials and tribulations
              Mehket: Piece the secrets hidden in the night
              Nosferotu: Strike terror into those who would harm me
              Ventue: Bend the insubordinate under my will

              Covenant Aspiration: (Static)
              Carthian: Protest, lobby, and make new policies to refine the politics of kindred society
              Circle of the Crone: Follow ancient pagan mysteries to revel in my Undeath
              Invictus: Enforce the age old feudal hierarchy that keeps kindred society functional
              Lance Sanctum: Find solace in serving my divinely provisioned role in God's plan
              Ordo Dracul: Study and research to refine my Curse to overcome its limitations

              Touchstone Aspiration (Vampire Only): This Aspiration is chosen individual character and always focuses on some goal with their touchstone. Vampire is a game of very personal horror and doesn't necessarily needs teamwork for that to take place. By mandating having possibly contradictory goals over fragile pawns outside of the characters themselves, it supports the themes of interpersonal intrigue and politicking.

              Stray Experience is earned from Stray Beats from pursuing Seeming, Universal Lost, and Court Aspirations

              Seeming Aspirations
              Beast Avoid confinement socially and physically at all costs.
              Darkling: Do the bad things necessary for those I care for so they don't have to.
              Elemental: Make sense of pure chaos caused by my own actions.
              Fairest: Lead those in need through times of crisis.
              Ogre: Make the world around me understand that sometimes violence is the only answer.
              Wizened: Escape my troubling thoughts through the sanctuary of my artistry.

              Universal Lost Aspiration
              All Lost by their very nature share this Aspiration, which at the same time is a source of great community and profound treachery among them.
              Lost (Changeling): Never be trapped in Arcadia again.

              Seasonal Courts
              Court Aspirations Examples (Each Court defines its Aspiration during its founding)
              Spring Court: Tend the flowers of desire within my own heart and the hearts of others.
              Summer Court: Scorch and bring to light like the unforgiving sun those that have wronged me and mine.
              Autumn: Reap the harvest of power gained by controlling fear.
              Winter: Hide my sorrows under the deepest frozen veils.

              Equal Experience Opportunities
              The following mechanics represent Aspiration-like mechanics that compel a character whether the character likes it or not. They are the itch that wishes to be scratched and is so satisfying to do so. If you are also using Shared Aspirations, please feel free to make them Shared Obsessions/Maneuvers/Instincts/Bewilderments/Succors. The intention is to provide every Major Template opportunities to explore sometimes neglected themes in their game and be rewarded for doing so.

              Obsessions
              Obsessions are just like long-term Aspirations, except they relate specifically to a mage’s compulsion to explore the mystical in her life. They could be goals to learn or research specific things. They could be player goals for the character to encounter certain strangeness in the world. They could be goals to use magic in new or extreme ways.
              Every Awakened character receives one or more Obses*sions. This depends on your character’s Gnosis dots. A character with one or two Gnosis dots has only one Obses*sion. A character with three, four, or five dots of Gnosis has two Obsessions. A character with six, seven, or eight dots of Gnosis has three Obsessions. And a character with nine or ten Gnosis has four Obsessions.
              In addition to the Arcane Beat from resolution, resolving an Obsession also gives the character a point of Mana.
              Additionally, Obsessions apply any time an Aspiration could apply. For example, they influence Social maneuvering actions. Any time a character uses Focused Mage Sight Scrutiny and an Obsession applies, the Obsession adds a die to the attempt.

              Maneuvers
              Maneuvers are just like long-term Aspirations, except they relate specifically to a vampire’s compulsions in how she spends her unlife in the Danse Macabre. They are repeated steps or moves that occur again and again, echoing through eternity. They could be goals in their petty cruelty when dealing with rivals or allies. They could be player goals for the character to experience certain types of tragedy. They could be goals to hold and maintain a certain position or keep their environment in a form that is familiar to them. Maneuvers essentially reflect a static nature and make vampires predictable, like knowing someone’s next move in an elaborate dance.
              Every Requiem character receives one or more Maneuvers. This depends on your character’s Blood Potency dots. A character with one Blood Potency dot has only one Maneuvers. A character with two or three dots of Blood Potency has two Maneuvers. A character with four, or five dots of Blood Potency has three Maneuvers.
              In addition to the Sanguine Beat from resolution, resolving a Maneuvers also gives the character a point of Willpower.
              Additionally, Maneuvers apply any time an Aspiration could apply. For example, they influence Social maneuvering actions. Any time a character Lashes Out with their Beast and a Maneuver applies, the Maneuver adds a die to the attempt.

              Instincts
              Instincts are just like long-term Aspirations, except they relate specifically to a werewolf’s compulsions during the Siskur-Dah. They are expressions of the werewolf’s most basic fleshly nature, either wolf or human. They could be goals in what they prioritize during the Siskur-Dah. They could be player goals for how the character reacts to certain types of prey they’ll encounter. They could be goals to how they interact with their pack mates. Instincts are reflexes that run deep in a character’s animalistic nature or human subconscious and if pursuing them is a breaking point it is always towards the flesh (or Against the Spirit if Frailty Chronicles rules apply).
              Every Werewolf character receives one or more Instincts. This depends on your character’s Primal Urge dots. A character with one Primal Urge dot has only one Instincts. A character with two or three dots of Primal Urge has two Instincts. A character with four, or five dots of Primal Urge has three Instincts.
              In addition to the Primal Beat from resolution, resolving a Instincts also gives the character a point of Essence.
              Additionally, Instincts apply any time an Aspiration could apply. For example, they influence Social maneuvering actions. Any time a character is actively on a Siskur-Dah and a Instinct applies, the Instinct adds a die to whatever the werewolf is doing.

              Bewilderments
              Bewilderments are just like long-term Aspirations, except they relate specifically to a changeling’s Arcadian trauma that gives them the impression of being ‘lost’. They are mental maladies resulting from experiencing things beyond human endurance and being transformed by them. They could be goals in deal with unreal problems as if they were life or death situations. They could be player goals for how the character reacts to certain reminders of their time in arcadia. They could be goals to interact in ways which are peculiar. Bewilderments should present a fantastic portrayal of madness reminiscent of fairy tales and/or Lovecraftian horror.
              Every Lost character receives one or more Bewilderments. This depends on your character’s Wyrd dots. A character with one Wyrd dot has only one Bewilderment. A character with two or three dots of Wyrd has two Bewilderments. A character with four, or five dots of Wyrd has three Bewilderments.
              In addition to the Stay Beat from resolution, resolving a Bewilderments also gives the character a point of Glamour.
              Additionally, Bewilderments apply any time an Aspiration could apply. For example, they influence Social maneuvering actions. Any time a character is actively on a invoking the Wyrd and a Bewilderments applies, the Bewilderment adds a die to whatever the changeling is doing.

              Succors
              Succors are just like long-term Aspirations, except they relate specifically to a sin-eater’s compulsions involving providing support surrounding death They are longings of the sin-eater to bring resolution concerning mortal matters. They could be goals in how they deal with the living left behind after a death. They could be player goals for how the character reacts to certain deathly phenomenon. They could be goals to how they interact with those who are already dead. Succors are always transference or projection of how a character’s inner feelings about death onto others and the outside world, and thus tend to at least seem sympathetic if viewed from the sin-eater’s perspective
              Every Sin-eater character receives one or more Succors. This depends on your character’s Psyche dots. A character with one Psyche dot has only one Succor. A character with two or three dots of Psyche has two Succors. A character with four, or five dots of Psyche has three Succors.
              In addition to the Ossuary Beat from resolution, resolving a Succors also gives the character a point of Plasm.
              Additionally, Succors apply any time an Aspiration could apply. For example, they influence Social maneuvering actions. Any time a character is actively on Manifesting and a Succor applies, the Succor adds a die to Manifestation.

              Experience Costs

              Attribute: 4 Fundamental Experiences
              Skill: 2 Fundamental Experiences

              Support: 2 Sane Experiences
              Willpower: 1 Sane Experience


              Merit: 1 Story Experience
              Skill Specialty: 1 Story Experience

              Gnosis: 5 Arcane Experiences
              Ruling Arcanum: 3 Arcane Experiences
              Common Arcanum: 4 Arcane Experiences
              Inferior Arcanum: 5 Arcane Experiences
              Rotes: 1 Arcane Experience

              Primal Urge: 5 Primal Experiences

              Blood Potency: 5 Sanguine Experiences

              Psyche: 5 Ossulary Experiences

              Wyrd: 5 Stray Experiences

              Choice of a Primal or Sanity Beat for scenes with Spirit Support.
